<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Jensen Huang has resigned. The available material does not provide reliable details about the timing, reasons, or succession.</span><span class='zh'>黄仁勋已辞职。现有材料没有提供有关时间、原因或继任安排的可靠细节。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Jensen Huang has resigned. The available material does not provide reliable details about the timing, reasons, or succession. The departure marks a leadership change at Nvidia.</span><span class='zh'>黄仁勋已辞职。现有材料没有提供有关时间、原因或继任安排的可靠细节。这意味着英伟达将发生领导层变动。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.cs.cmu.edu/learn/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Carnegie Mellon is putting computer science courses on YouTube for free</span><span class='zh'>卡内基梅隆大学将计算机科学课程免费放到 YouTube</span></a><span class="dom">cs.cmu.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Carnegie Mellon’s Kenneth C. Griffin School of Computer Science is publishing free lectures on YouTube.</span><span class='zh'>卡内基梅隆大学肯尼斯·C·格里芬计算机科学学院正在 YouTube 上发布免费课程讲座。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Carnegie Mellon’s Kenneth C. Griffin School of Computer Science is publishing free lectures on YouTube. Self-paced courseware is planned as the next step. The release gives learners access to the school’s computer science material without charge.</span><span class='zh'>卡内基梅隆大学肯尼斯·C·格里芬计算机科学学院正在 YouTube 上发布免费课程讲座。下一步计划推出可自主学习的课程材料。这将让学习者免费接触该学院的计算机科学内容。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.foldableflight.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Foldable Flight: The Best Paper Airplanes</span><span class='zh'>折叠飞行：最佳纸飞机</span></a><span class="dom">foldableflight.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The site presents a large database of paper airplanes with step-by-step video folding tutorials.</span><span class='zh'>该网站提供大型纸飞机数据库，并为每种机型配备分步折叠视频教程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The site presents a large database of paper airplanes with step-by-step video folding tutorials. One World Record prototype is reported to fly more than 300 feet. The Unite model holds a world record for time aloft at 31.2 seconds.</span><span class='zh'>该网站提供大型纸飞机数据库，并为每种机型配备分步折叠视频教程。据称，一款世界纪录原型机可以飞行超过 300 英尺。Unite 机型则以 31.2 秒的滞空时间保持世界纪录。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">07</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nytimes.com/2026/11/17/upshot/school-phone-bans-study-scores.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>New Evidence Shows School Phone Bans Are Gradually Improving Test Scores</span><span class='zh'>新证据显示，学校禁用手机正逐步提高考试成绩</span></a><span class="dom">nytimes.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A large national analysis comparing students’ scores before and after cellphone bans found modest academic improvements.</span><span class='zh'>一项比较学生禁用手机前后成绩的大型全国性分析发现，学业表现出现小幅改善。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A large national analysis comparing students’ scores before and after cellphone bans found modest academic improvements. Schools with strict policies requiring students to lock phones in pouches for the entire school day saw math and reading gains equivalent to roughly one or two additional weeks of schooling per year. The study was published by Harvard’s Center for Education Policy Research and is the first national study to track individual students’ growth in this way.</span><span class='zh'>一项比较学生禁用手机前后成绩的大型全国性分析发现，学业表现出现小幅改善。要求学生在整个上课日将手机锁入袋中的学校，数学和阅读成绩提升幅度相当于每年额外增加约一至两周的学习。该研究由哈佛教育政策研究中心发布，是首项以这种方式追踪个体学生成长的全国性研究。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Education' style='--c:#60a5fa'>Education</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/17/top_stories_11172026'><span class='en'>Nov 17</span><span class='zh'>11月17日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>7.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>1169</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50530163' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>582</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50471731" data-id="50471731" data-tags="DevOps" data-score="1161" data-comments="978" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50471731" data-time="1794507648">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://status.cloud.microsoft/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Service degradation on Microsoft 365</span><span class='zh'>Microsoft 365 服务降级</span></a><span class="dom">status.cloud.microsoft</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft 365 is experiencing service degradation.</span><span class='zh'>Microsoft 365 正在经历服务降级。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft 365 is experiencing service degradation. The available material does not specify which services are affected, the cause, or the expected resolution time. Users may therefore encounter disruptions while the incident is being addressed.</span><span class='zh'>Microsoft 365 正在经历服务降级。现有材料没有说明受影响的具体服务、原因或预计恢复时间。因此，用户在问题处理期间可能会遇到服务中断。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">09</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://docodethatmatters.com/home-automation-overnight-success/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Smart home devices should work without the internet</span><span class='zh'>智能家居应当无需互联网也能运行</span></a><span class="dom">docodethatmatters.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author describes a decade-long home-automation rule: no core function in the house should depend on a vendor’s server.</span><span class='zh'>作者介绍了坚持十年的家庭自动化原则：家中的核心功能不应依赖厂商服务器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author describes a decade-long home-automation rule: no core function in the house should depend on a vendor’s server. As a result, the home continues operating when the internet goes down. The post presents the other automation rules and two related talks that grew out of this principle.</span><span class='zh'>作者介绍了坚持十年的家庭自动化原则：家中的核心功能不应依赖厂商服务器。因此，即使互联网中断，房屋仍能继续运行。文章还整理了由这一原则延伸出的其他自动化规则和两场相关演讲。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/10/top_stories_11102026'><span class='en'>Nov 10</span><span class='zh'>11月10日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>1142</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50441707' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>432</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50507303" data-id="50507303" data-tags="AI|Open Source" data-score="1094" data-comments="595" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50507303" data-time="1794754018">
<a class="thumb" href="https://jakub.jankiewicz.org/blog/ai-code-open-source/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://jakub.jankiewicz.org/img/en/ai-code-open-source.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='jakub.jankiewicz.org' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">10</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://jakub.jankiewicz.org/blog/ai-code-open-source/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>All AI-generated code should be Open Source</span><span class='zh'>所有 AI 生成的代码都应开源</span></a><span class="dom">jakub.jankiewicz.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author argues that all code generated with AI assistance should be released as open source or under a CC0 license.</span><span class='zh'>作者主张，所有借助 AI 生成的代码都应以开源许可证或 CC0 许可发布。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author argues that all code generated with AI assistance should be released as open source or under a CC0 license. The argument is based on AI models being trained on large amounts of publicly available open-source code, as well as Stack Overflow and related sites whose Creative Commons licenses generally require attribution. The author says turning that shared knowledge into proprietary code resembles license laundering.</span><span class='zh'>作者主张，所有借助 AI 生成的代码都应以开源许可证或 CC0 许可发布。其理由是，AI 模型训练使用了大量公开的开源代码，以及通常要求署名的 Stack Overflow 和相关网站内容。作者认为，将这些共享知识转化为专有闭源代码近似于“许可证洗白”。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/15/top_stories_11152026'><span class='en'>Nov 15</span><span class='zh'>11月15日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>10.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>1094</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50507303' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>595</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50535230" data-id="50535230" data-tags="AI" data-score="1086" data-comments="698" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50535230" data-time="1794937603">

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GPT-6.2 Astra and Sol are presented as two named models or systems. No further technical details or capabilities are established here.</span><span class='zh'>GPT‑6.2 Astra 和 Sol 是两个名为 Astra 与 Sol 的模型或系统。现有信息未提供更多技术细节或功能说明。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.techpowerup.com/354814/nvidia-allegedly-engineered-scarcity-by-funding-ai-startups-to-buy-its-gpus" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.techpowerup.com/img/ooCln2q3jkKNGHen.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='techpowerup.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">12</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.techpowerup.com/354814/nvidia-allegedly-engineered-scarcity-by-funding-ai-startups-to-buy-its-gpus" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia allegedly engineered scarcity by funding AI startups to buy its GPUs</span><span class='zh'>英伟达被指通过资助 AI 初创公司购买 GPU，人为制造供应紧张</span></a><span class="dom">techpowerup.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A new report, citing The Wall Street Journal, alleges that NVIDIA increased the GPU and memory shortage by investing in AI startups that then bought NVIDIA GPUs and built data centers.</span><span class='zh'>一份援引《华尔街日报》的新报告称，英伟达通过投资 AI 初创公司，促使这些公司购买英伟达 GPU 并建设数据中心，从而加剧了 GPU 和内存短缺。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A new report, citing The Wall Street Journal, alleges that NVIDIA increased the GPU and memory shortage by investing in AI startups that then bought NVIDIA GPUs and built data centers. The allegation emerged after Jensen Huang’s departure as NVIDIA’s CEO, according to the supplied report. The claim concerns whether the company’s financing activity helped inflate demand for its own hardware.</span><span class='zh'>一份援引《华尔街日报》的新报告称，英伟达通过投资 AI 初创公司，促使这些公司购买英伟达 GPU 并建设数据中心，从而加剧了 GPU 和内存短缺。报告称，这一指控是在 Jensen Huang 离任英伟达 CEO 后出现的。争议焦点在于，英伟达的融资活动是否帮助推高了对其自有硬件的需求。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/14/top_stories_11142026'><span class='en'>Nov 14</span><span class='zh'>11月14日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>3.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>1036</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50493251' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>621</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50452455" data-id="50452455" data-tags="AI|Science|Space|Politics" data-score="1027" data-comments="390" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50452455" data-time="1794398722">
<a class="thumb" href="https://pietromiotti.com/latest/maya-site" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://pietromiotti.com/latest/maya-site/forest-vs-lidar.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='pietromiotti.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">13</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://pietromiotti.com/latest/maya-site" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>My AI agents found an unmapped Maya site?</span><span class='zh'>我的 AI 代理发现了一个未标记的玛雅遗址？</span></a><span class="dom">pietromiotti.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI agents analyzed NASA lidar data and identified a Maya site beneath the jungle in Mexico.</span><span class='zh'>AI 代理分析 NASA 的激光雷达数据，在墨西哥丛林下识别出一处玛雅遗址。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI agents analyzed NASA lidar data and identified a Maya site beneath the jungle in Mexico. The site had already been mapped by archaeologists and described in Estrada-Belli et al. 2025, but it was not yet listed in Mexico’s official INAH register. The agents therefore rediscovered the site rather than discovering it, and the process took less than 72 hours to reach an official response from the Mexican government.</span><span class='zh'>AI 代理分析 NASA 的激光雷达数据，在墨西哥丛林下识别出一处玛雅遗址。考古学家其实已经绘制过该遗址的地图，并在 Estrada-Belli 等人 2025 年的论文中记录了它，只是它尚未列入墨西哥国家人类学与历史研究所（INAH）的官方名录。因此，代理完成的是“重新发现”而非发现；从提出想法到收到墨西哥政府的正式回复耗时不到 72 小时。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span><span class='tag' data-t='Space' style='--c:#818cf8'>Space</span><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>1027</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50452455' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>390</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50465829" data-id="50465829" data-tags="Business|Finance|AI|Science" data-score="996" data-comments="904" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50465829" data-time="1794484001">
<a class="thumb" href="https://ipo.anthropic.com/us/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://ipo.anthropic.com/assets/share-image-eAr8nJRP.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='ipo.anthropic.com' data-c='#0ea5e9' data-tag='Business'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">14</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ipo.anthropic.com/us/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Anthropic IPO</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 筹备首次公开募股</span></a><span class="dom">ipo.anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic has filed to become a public company and published a founders’ letter along with related information.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 已提交申请，准备成为一家上市公司，并公布了创始人公开信及相关信息。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic has filed to become a public company and published a founders’ letter along with related information. The company is organized as a public benefit corporation focused on securing AI’s benefits and mitigating its risks. It says AI could accelerate scientific innovation, increase economic growth, help cure major diseases, and address long-standing problems if managed well.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 已提交申请，准备成为一家上市公司，并公布了创始人公开信及相关信息。该公司采用公益公司架构，致力于发挥人工智能的益处并降低其风险。Anthropic 表示，如果得到妥善管理，人工智能可能加速科学创新、提高经济增长率、推动重大疾病治疗，并解决长期未能解决的问题。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span><span class='tag' data-t='Finance' style='--c:#84cc16'>Finance</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>996</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50465829' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>904</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50499017" data-id="50499017" data-tags="Privacy|Startups|AI" data-score="974" data-comments="372" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50499017" data-time="1794678190">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.crissyfield.de/research/lovable-en/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.crissyfield.de/images/research-lovable-opengraph.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='crissyfield.de' data-c='#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">15</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.crissyfield.de/research/lovable-en/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Lovable apps expose passport details and more than 200K private messages</span><span class='zh'>Lovable 应用暴露护照信息和超过 20 万条私人消息</span></a><span class="dom">crissyfield.de</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An analysis of 159,041 Lovable projects found many cases in which personal and confidential information was accessible without logging in, including passport details and more than 200,000 private messages.</span><span class='zh'>对 159,041 个 Lovable 项目的分析发现，许多项目无需登录即可访问个人和机密信息，其中包括护照详情以及超过 20 万条私人消息。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An analysis of 159,041 Lovable projects found many cases in which personal and confidential information was accessible without logging in, including passport details and more than 200,000 private messages. Lovable is a Stockholm-based Swedish AI startup founded in 2023 by Anton Osika and Fabian Hedin, whose platform generates web applications from plain-language descriptions. The findings show that building applications with AI does not remove the need for careful handling of sensitive data.</span><span class='zh'>对 159,041 个 Lovable 项目的分析发现，许多项目无需登录即可访问个人和机密信息，其中包括护照详情以及超过 20 万条私人消息。Lovable 是一家总部位于斯德哥尔摩、由 Anton Osika 和 Fabian Hedin 于 2023 年创立的瑞典 AI 初创公司，其平台可根据自然语言描述生成 Web 应用。这些发现表明，使用 AI 构建应用并不能免除对敏感数据进行谨慎处理的要求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="http://groundworkcollaborative.org/news/amazons-decoy-deals-groundwork-investigation-reveals-nearly-1-in-3-black-friday-and-cyber-monday-deals-advertised-by-amazon-offered-no-savings-to-shoppers-nearly-1-in-10-w/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Many of Amazon’s Black Friday and Cyber Monday “deals” offered no savings</span><span class='zh'>亚马逊许多黑色星期五和网络星期一“优惠”并没有省钱</span></a><span class="dom">groundworkcollaborative.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An investigation found that many Black Friday and Cyber Monday deals advertised by Amazon did not provide actual savings.</span><span class='zh'>一项调查发现，亚马逊宣传的许多黑色星期五和网络星期一优惠实际上并没有带来节省。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An investigation found that many Black Friday and Cyber Monday deals advertised by Amazon did not provide actual savings. The deals were presented as discounts even though shoppers often paid no less than usual.</span><span class='zh'>一项调查发现，亚马逊宣传的许多黑色星期五和网络星期一优惠实际上并没有带来节省。这些商品虽然以折扣形式展示，消费者实际支付的价格却往往并未降低。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://neil.fraser.name/news/2026/11/10/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>.name Grandfathered</span><span class='zh'>.name 域名获得保留</span></a><span class="dom">neil.fraser.name</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Verisign has withdrawn its plan to terminate 22,000 .name domain names and email redirects after a prolonged campaign.</span><span class='zh'>经过一场旷日持久的抗争，Verisign 撤回了终止 22,000 个 .name 域名及其邮件转发的计划。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Verisign has withdrawn its plan to terminate 22,000 .name domain names and email redirects after a prolonged campaign. New registrations for third-level .name domains will close, but all existing domains and redirects will remain indefinitely. The reversal followed efforts by domain owners, lawyers, and supporters to challenge the plan through ICANN.</span><span class='zh'>经过一场旷日持久的抗争，Verisign 撤回了终止 22,000 个 .name 域名及其邮件转发的计划。虽然 .name 三级域名将停止新注册，但现有域名和邮件转发服务将无限期保留。这一转变源于域名所有者、律师和支持者通过 ICANN 发起的共同争取。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Rust team has released Rust 1.100.0.</span><span class='zh'>Rust 团队发布了 Rust 1.100.0。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Rust team has released Rust 1.100.0. Rust is described as a language for building reliable and efficient software. Users with an existing rustup installation can install the new version through rustup.</span><span class='zh'>Rust 团队发布了 Rust 1.100.0。Rust 被定位为用于构建可靠、高效软件的编程语言。已经通过 rustup 安装 Rust 的用户可以使用 rustup 获取新版本。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.wired.com/story/nvidia-employees-petition-for-jensen-huangs-return/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia employees petition to bring back Jensen Huang</span><span class='zh'>英伟达员工请愿要求让黄仁勋回归</span></a><span class="dom">wired.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>More than 1,600 comments, mostly supportive, have appeared on an internal Nvidia post urging the board to consider employee sentiment about former CEO Jensen Huang.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达内部一则呼吁董事会考虑员工对前 CEO 黄仁勋看法的帖子已收到超过 1,600 条评论，其中大多数表示支持。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>More than 1,600 comments, mostly supportive, have appeared on an internal Nvidia post urging the board to consider employee sentiment about former CEO Jensen Huang. The post was submitted before Huang’s resignation by a longtime engineer and asked the board to consider employee views before taking action. Some employees are now asking the board to find a way to bring Huang back despite reports of multiple extramarital affairs and his abrupt resignation.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达内部一则呼吁董事会考虑员工对前 CEO 黄仁勋看法的帖子已收到超过 1,600 条评论，其中大多数表示支持。该帖子由一名长期任职的工程师在黄仁勋辞职前提交，要求董事会在采取行动前考虑员工意见。尽管有报道称黄仁勋存在多段婚外情并突然辞职，仍有部分员工要求董事会设法让他回归。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://scottjg.com/posts/2026-11-11-reverse-engineering-my-coway-air-purifier-to-ditch-the-cloud/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Reverse engineering my Coway air purifier to ditch the cloud</span><span class='zh'>逆向工程我的 Coway 空气净化器，摆脱云端控制</span></a><span class="dom">scottjg.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>During the 2017 wave of annual wildfires on the West Coast, many Bay Area residents bought air purifiers because their homes were poorly insulated and sealed.</span><span class='zh'>2017 年西海岸接连发生野火时，许多湾区居民因为房屋隔热和密封性能较差而购买空气净化器，以避免吸入野火烟尘。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>During the 2017 wave of annual wildfires on the West Coast, many Bay Area residents bought air purifiers because their homes were poorly insulated and sealed. The author describes buying a Coway Airmega to avoid breathing wildfire soot and reverse-engineering it to remove its dependence on cloud services. The project reflects a broader effort to keep household devices locally controlled.</span><span class='zh'>2017 年西海岸接连发生野火时，许多湾区居民因为房屋隔热和密封性能较差而购买空气净化器，以避免吸入野火烟尘。作者介绍了自己购买 Coway Airmega，并对其进行逆向工程，以摆脱对云端服务的依赖。这一项目体现了将家用设备控制权保留在本地的尝试。</span></p>

The available description does not provide further technical details about the machine or the application.</span><span class='zh'>一名开发者为一台需要订阅的 300 美元刺绣机编写了替代应用。该项目以 Zigzag 的形式发布。现有描述没有提供有关机器或应用的更多技术细节。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">28</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://xusheng.dev/posts/windows-coding-agent-telemetry/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Windows 11 is collecting data on which coding agents and developer tools you use</span><span class='zh'>Windows 11 正收集你使用哪些编程代理和开发工具的数据</span></a><span class="dom">xusheng.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Reverse engineering of Windows 11’s MissionControlAggregator found coding-agent and developer-tool usage bits in a diagnostic ETW report.</span><span class='zh'>对 Windows 11 的 MissionControlAggregator 进行逆向分析后，研究者在诊断 ETW 报告中发现了编程代理和开发工具的使用标记。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Reverse engineering of Windows 11’s MissionControlAggregator found coding-agent and developer-tool usage bits in a diagnostic ETW report. The observed list includes Codex, Claude, Copilot, Cursor, Python, .NET, GCC, and Visual Studio Code executables. The finding raises questions about why a Windows system DLL records the use of these tools.</span><span class='zh'>对 Windows 11 的 MissionControlAggregator 进行逆向分析后，研究者在诊断 ETW 报告中发现了编程代理和开发工具的使用标记。相关列表包括 Codex、Claude、Copilot、Cursor，以及 Python、.NET、GCC 和 Visual Studio Code 等可执行文件。这一发现引出了一个问题：Windows 系统 DLL 为何要记录这些工具的使用情况。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/16/top_stories_11162026'><span class='en'>Nov 16</span><span class='zh'>11月16日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>711</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50524736' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>592</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50524767" data-id="50524767" data-tags="Security|Programming|Finance" data-score="669" data-comments="264" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50524767" data-time="1794870034">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.report-uri.com/worlds-largest-brands-infected-with-malware-via-optimizely/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Optimizely compromised, served malware on Zoom, GitLab</span><span class='zh'>Optimizely 遭入侵，Zoom、GitLab 等网站被投放恶意软件</span></a><span class="dom">blog.report-uri.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>On November 16, 2026, malicious JavaScript was served through Optimizely’s Web experimentation platform to visitors of 178 organizations, including Zoom, the BBC, CNN, Salesforce, GitLab, IKEA, HSBC, and others.</span><span class='zh'>2026 年 11 月 16 日，Optimizely 的 Web 实验平台向 178 家机构的网站访客投放了恶意 JavaScript，受影响机构包括 Zoom、BBC、CNN、Salesforce、GitLab、IKEA 和 HSBC 等。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>On November 16, 2026, malicious JavaScript was served through Optimizely’s Web experimentation platform to visitors of 178 organizations, including Zoom, the BBC, CNN, Salesforce, GitLab, IKEA, HSBC, and others. The malware could replace cryptocurrency wallet addresses, skim payment-card data, steal cookies and screenshots, and deliver the Coruna and DarkSword iOS exploit kits on iPhones and iPads. Cryptocurrency had already been stolen in transactions verified as belonging to the attackers.</span><span class='zh'>2026 年 11 月 16 日，Optimizely 的 Web 实验平台向 178 家机构的网站访客投放了恶意 JavaScript，受影响机构包括 Zoom、BBC、CNN、Salesforce、GitLab、IKEA 和 HSBC 等。该恶意软件能够替换加密货币钱包地址、窃取支付卡数据、Cookie 和屏幕截图，并在 iPhone 和 iPad 上投递 Coruna 与 DarkSword iOS 漏洞利用套件。已有加密货币在被确认属于攻击者的交易中遭窃取。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Finance' style='--c:#84cc16'>Finance</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/16/top_stories_11162026'><span class='en'>Nov 16</span><span class='zh'>11月16日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>669</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50524767' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>264</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50458900" data-id="50458900" data-tags="AI|Security" data-score="661" data-comments="688" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50458900" data-time="1794432275">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.cbsnews.com/news/united-airlines-bans-ai-agents-booking-flights/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>United Airlines bans AI agents from booking flights on behalf of travelers</span><span class='zh'>联合航空禁止 AI 代理代旅客预订航班</span></a><span class="dom">cbsnews.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>United Airlines said it is banning AI agents from making flight reservations on behalf of travelers.</span><span class='zh'>联合航空表示，将禁止 AI 代理代旅客预订航班。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>United Airlines said it is banning AI agents from making flight reservations on behalf of travelers. The airline cited security risks and the possibility that such actions could violate federal rules. Consumers are increasingly using agents such as Meta’s Muse and OpenAI’s dots for tasks including shopping and purchases.</span><span class='zh'>联合航空表示，将禁止 AI 代理代旅客预订航班。该航空公司称，这类操作存在安全风险，并可能违反联邦法规。消费者正越来越多地使用 Meta 的 Muse 和 OpenAI 的 dots 等代理来完成商品研究、比价和购买等任务。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>661</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50458900' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>688</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50438261" data-id="50438261" data-tags="Education|AI|Code" data-score="655" data-comments="155" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50438261" data-time="1794305902">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/janestreet/agent_skills/blob/master/socratic-tutor/SKILL.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#60a5fa' data-tag='Education'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">31</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/janestreet/agent_skills/blob/master/socratic-tutor/SKILL.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Socratic Tutor</span><span class='zh'>苏格拉底式导师</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Socratic Tutor is an agent skill designed to guide users toward genuine mastery through questions that make them reason, predict, and explain rather than simply listen to a lecture.</span><span class='zh'>“苏格拉底式导师”是一项代理技能，通过要求用户推理、预测和解释来帮助其真正掌握知识，而不是单纯进行讲授。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Socratic Tutor is an agent skill designed to guide users toward genuine mastery through questions that make them reason, predict, and explain rather than simply listen to a lecture. It is intended for ongoing teaching sessions, not one-off factual questions. When the user’s intent is ambiguous, it first asks whether they want a quick answer or a full tutoring session.</span><span class='zh'>“苏格拉底式导师”是一项代理技能，通过要求用户推理、预测和解释来帮助其真正掌握知识，而不是单纯进行讲授。它适用于持续的教学过程，不适用于一次性的事实问答。当用户意图不明确时，它会先询问用户想要快速答案还是完整的辅导过程。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Education' style='--c:#60a5fa'>Education</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>655</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50438261' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>155</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50535238" data-id="50535238" data-tags="Web" data-score="631" data-comments="352" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50535238" data-time="1794937634">
<a class="thumb" href="https://developer.apple.com/system-status/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#06b6d4' data-tag='Web'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=developer.apple.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>developer.apple.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">32</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://developer.apple.com/system-status/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Apple Services Down</span><span class='zh'>Apple 服务中断</span></a><span class="dom">developer.apple.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple services are reported to be down.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，Apple 服务发生中断。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple services are reported to be down. The available information does not identify which services are affected, how long the outage has lasted, or its cause.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，Apple 服务发生中断。现有信息未说明受影响的具体服务、持续时间或原因。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www1.wdr.de/nrw/rheinland/rhein-erft-kreis/technischer-defekt-chlor-phantasialand-100.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microsoft 365 outage leads to chlorine gas poisoning of 15 hotel guests</span><span class='zh'>Microsoft 365 故障导致 15 名酒店客人氯气中毒</span></a><span class="dom">www1.wdr.de</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Microsoft Outlook outage meant Phantasialand did not receive notice that excessive chlorine was present in a pool.</span><span class='zh'>微软 Outlook 服务中断，导致 Phantasialand 未能收到泳池氯含量过高的通知。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Microsoft Outlook outage meant Phantasialand did not receive notice that excessive chlorine was present in a pool. Fifteen guests were injured as a result.</span><span class='zh'>微软 Outlook 服务中断，导致 Phantasialand 未能收到泳池氯含量过高的通知。结果造成 15 名客人受伤。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://so.long.thanks.fish/why-pi-when-i-have-an-old-phone/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why get an expensive Pi when I have an old phone?</span><span class='zh'>既然有旧手机，为什么还要买昂贵的树莓派？</span></a><span class="dom">so.long.thanks.fish</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project uses a rooted second-hand Redmi smartphone as a camera and light controller instead of building the system around a Raspberry Pi.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目改用一部已取得 root 权限的二手 Redmi 手机来充当摄像头和灯光控制器，而不是围绕树莓派搭建系统。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project uses a rooted second-hand Redmi smartphone as a camera and light controller instead of building the system around a Raspberry Pi. The phone already provides a high-resolution camera, GPS, storage, battery, and mobile data, while the original Raspberry Pi design required adding an Arducam 64MP camera and a GPS module. The approach could reduce the number of components and the project’s growing bill.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目改用一部已取得 root 权限的二手 Redmi 手机来充当摄像头和灯光控制器，而不是围绕树莓派搭建系统。手机已经具备高分辨率摄像头、GPS、存储、电池和移动数据功能；原先的树莓派方案则需要额外加入 Arducam 6400 万像素摄像头和 GPS 模块。这种做法有望减少组件数量，并控制不断上涨的项目成本。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Energy' style='--c:#eab308'>Energy</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/14/top_stories_11142026'><span class='en'>Nov 14</span><span class='zh'>11月14日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>617</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50440709' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>322</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50513160" data-id="50513160" data-tags="Security|Programming" data-score="598" data-comments="174" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50513160" data-time="1794794688">
<a class="thumb" href="https://iter.ca/post/ubo-uxss/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://iter.ca//ubo-epicker.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='iter.ca' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://iter.ca/post/ubo-uxss/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Universal XSS in uBlock Origin from website-driven element picker</span><span class='zh'>uBlock Origin 的网站驱动元素选择器存在通用 XSS</span></a><span class="dom">iter.ca</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A one-click universal XSS vulnerability affected the Firefox version of uBlock Origin through its element picker.</span><span class='zh'>uBlock Origin 的 Firefox 版本元素选择器存在一个可通过一次点击触发的通用 XSS 漏洞。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A one-click universal XSS vulnerability affected the Firefox version of uBlock Origin through its element picker. A malicious website could trick the extension into adding arbitrary trusted filters, which can inject JavaScript into other websites and execute it in their contexts. The issue has been fixed; a related but less severe problem also affected the MV3-based uBO Lite.</span><span class='zh'>uBlock Origin 的 Firefox 版本元素选择器存在一个可通过一次点击触发的通用 XSS 漏洞。恶意网站可以诱使扩展添加任意受信过滤规则，而受信规则能够向其他网站注入 JavaScript，并在这些网站的上下文中执行。该问题现已修复；基于 MV3 的 uBO Lite 也受到一个相关但危害较小的问题影响。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/16/top_stories_11162026'><span class='en'>Nov 16</span><span class='zh'>11月16日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>598</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50513160' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>174</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50455394" data-id="50455394" data-tags="DevOps" data-score="575" data-comments="272" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50455394" data-time="1794415124">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://michael.bacarella.com/2026/11/10/false-smoke-alarm-jane-street-datacenter/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A false smoke alarm almost drowned Jane Street&#x27;s only NYC datacenter</span><span class='zh'>一次误报的烟雾警报差点淹没 Jane Street 在纽约唯一的数据中心</span></a><span class="dom">michael.bacarella.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Jane Street systems-team account describes a false smoke alarm at the firm’s only New York datacenter.</span><span class='zh'>Jane Street 系统团队的一篇回忆讲述了该公司纽约唯一数据中心发生误报烟雾警报的经过。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Jane Street systems-team account describes a false smoke alarm at the firm’s only New York datacenter. The facility lost cooling, and staff rushed to unplug every power cord before the sprinkler system activated. The incident is presented as an example of the hands-on work required when a serious infrastructure failure nearly occurred.</span><span class='zh'>Jane Street 系统团队的一篇回忆讲述了该公司纽约唯一数据中心发生误报烟雾警报的经过。机房一度失去冷却，员工赶在喷淋系统启动前冲进去拔掉所有电源线。这个事件展示了基础设施险些发生严重故障时，系统团队必须亲自处理现场问题。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>575</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50455394' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>272</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50459745" data-id="50459745" data-tags="Programming" data-score="566" data-comments="79" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50459745" data-time="1794436463">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.mcmillen.dev/sigbovik/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=mcmillen.dev&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>mcmillen.dev</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">37</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.mcmillen.dev/sigbovik/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>93% of paint splatters are valid Perl programs (2019)</span><span class='zh'>93% 的颜料飞溅都是有效的 Perl 程序（2019）</span></a><span class="dom">mcmillen.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A paper investigates whether it is possible to smear paint on a wall without producing a valid Perl program.</span><span class='zh'>一篇论文研究了这样的问题：把颜料涂抹到墙上时，是否可能不生成有效的 Perl 程序。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A paper investigates whether it is possible to smear paint on a wall without producing a valid Perl program. Its stated result is that 93% of paint splatters are valid Perl programs, accompanied by a gallery of the resulting programs. The work presents the question as a long-standing open problem in programming languages.</span><span class='zh'>一篇论文研究了这样的问题：把颜料涂抹到墙上时，是否可能不生成有效的 Perl 程序。论文给出的结果是，93% 的颜料飞溅都属于有效 Perl 程序，并配有这些程序的展示画廊。作者把这个问题称为编程语言领域长期存在的开放问题。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>566</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50459745' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>79</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50496976" data-id="50496976" data-tags="Open Source|Culture" data-score="562" data-comments="133" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50496976" data-time="1794663808">
<a class="thumb" href="https://tube.kockatoo.org/w/fFinNHfanLT7aBEb2B8y4c" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://tube.kockatoo.org/lazy-static/thumbnails/02935b80-28d9-4fd7-bc33-9d0cb76f2e80.webp' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='tube.kockatoo.org' data-c='#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://tube.kockatoo.org/w/fFinNHfanLT7aBEb2B8y4c" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>KDE: The documentary [video]</span><span class='zh'>KDE：纪录片 [视频]</span></a><span class="dom">tube.kockatoo.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>KDE: The Documentary tells the story of the open-source community that emerged in 1996 to offer computer users a free alternative to proprietary desktops such as Windows.</span><span class='zh'>《KDE：纪录片》讲述了这一开源社区的故事：它于 1996 年兴起，为计算机用户提供了 Windows 等专有桌面之外的免费替代方案。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>KDE: The Documentary tells the story of the open-source community that emerged in 1996 to offer computer users a free alternative to proprietary desktops such as Windows. It traces the origins and development of KDE as a pioneering desktop project.</span><span class='zh'>《KDE：纪录片》讲述了这一开源社区的故事：它于 1996 年兴起，为计算机用户提供了 Windows 等专有桌面之外的免费替代方案。影片追溯了 KDE 这一先驱桌面项目的起源与发展。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Culture' style='--c:#d946ef'>Culture</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/14/top_stories_11142026'><span class='en'>Nov 14</span><span class='zh'>11月14日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>562</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50496976' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>133</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50471431" data-id="50471431" data-tags="AI" data-score="559" data-comments="349" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50471431" data-time="1794506508">
<a class="thumb" href="https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/models-and-research/gemini-models/introducing-gemini-4-hydrogen/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/gemini-4-hydrogen__key-art__lockup__16-9.width-1300.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='blog.google' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/models-and-research/gemini-models/introducing-gemini-4-hydrogen/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Gemini 4 Hydrogen</span><span class='zh'>Gemini 4 Hydrogen</span></a><span class="dom">blog.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google has introduced Gemini 4 Hydrogen as a fast, cost-efficient model for agentic workflows and everyday tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Google 推出了 Gemini 4 Hydrogen，将其定位为面向智能体工作流和日常任务的快速、高性价比模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google has introduced Gemini 4 Hydrogen as a fast, cost-efficient model for agentic workflows and everyday tasks. The company positions it as a daily-use model for reliable agentic work. Google also says Hydrogen brings advanced intelligence to users broadly.</span><span class='zh'>Google 推出了 Gemini 4 Hydrogen，将其定位为面向智能体工作流和日常任务的快速、高性价比模型。公司将其定位为可日常使用、适合可靠智能体工作的模型。Google 还表示，Hydrogen 将先进的智能能力带给更广泛的用户。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>559</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50471431' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>349</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50456791" data-id="50456791" data-tags="AI" data-score="554" data-comments="157" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50456791" data-time="1794421962">
<a class="thumb" href="https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/technology/developers-tools/gemini-decisions-api/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/gemini__decisions__blog-header_2784x1566.width-1300.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='blog.google' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">40</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/technology/developers-tools/gemini-decisions-api/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Gemini Decisions API</span><span class='zh'>Gemini Decisions API</span></a><span class="dom">blog.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google introduced the Gemini Decisions API, powered by its Gemini Decision 1 model.</span><span class='zh'>Google 发布了由 Gemini Decision 1 模型驱动的 Gemini Decisions API。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google introduced the Gemini Decisions API, powered by its Gemini Decision 1 model. It can evaluate up to 1,000 yes/no, multiple-choice, or rubric-scored questions in a single pass across text, images, audio, video, and PDFs. Google describes the decisions as fast and calibrated for its applications.</span><span class='zh'>Google 发布了由 Gemini Decision 1 模型驱动的 Gemini Decisions API。它可以在一次处理中，针对文本、图像、音频、视频和 PDF，评估最多 1,000 个是非题、选择题或按评分标准判定的问题。Google 将其描述为速度快且经过校准的决策 API。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theautopian.com/scion-is-back-and-its-going-to-sell-a-freaking-kei-truck-in-america/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Scion is back and is going to sell a kei truck in America</span><span class='zh'>Scion 回归，将在美国销售一款 Kei 微型卡车</span></a><span class="dom">theautopian.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Toyota has officially revived the Scion marque, which was retired in 2016 after years of financial struggles.</span><span class='zh'>丰田正式复活了 Scion 品牌；该品牌曾因长期经营困难于 2016 年停运。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Toyota has officially revived the Scion marque, which was retired in 2016 after years of financial struggles. The new “Scion by Toyota” is being positioned as a “freedom lab” focused on challenging conventional automotive thinking, and its initial offerings include a kei truck, a bike, and a hybrid side-by-side. The brand’s new direction is a sharp departure from its former role as a quirky, budget-oriented line aimed largely at younger buyers.</span><span class='zh'>丰田正式复活了 Scion 品牌；该品牌曾因长期经营困难于 2016 年停运。全新的“Scion by Toyota”被定位为一个挑战传统汽车思维的“自由实验室”，首批产品包括一款 Kei 微型卡车、一辆自行车和一款混合动力并排式越野车。品牌的新方向与过去面向年轻消费者、主打古怪且价格亲民车型的定位大相径庭。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://9to5mac.com/2026/11/10/apple-has-new-mac-app-coming-to-make-running-local-ai-models-easy/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Apple has new Mac app coming to make running local AI models easy</span><span class='zh'>Apple 将推出让本地运行 AI 模型更简单的 Mac 应用</span></a><span class="dom">9to5mac.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple is reportedly developing a Mac app called Harbor to simplify running local AI models across one or more Macs.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，Apple 正在开发一款名为 Harbor 的 Mac 应用，用于简化在一台或多台 Mac 上运行本地 AI 模型的过程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple is reportedly developing a Mac app called Harbor to simplify running local AI models across one or more Macs. Evidence of the app was found by leaker Aaron Perris. The project comes as Macs are increasingly being used to run models locally, including on the Mac Studio and Mac mini.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，Apple 正在开发一款名为 Harbor 的 Mac 应用，用于简化在一台或多台 Mac 上运行本地 AI 模型的过程。泄密者 Aaron Perris 发现了这款应用的相关证据。随着 Mac Studio、Mac mini 等设备被越来越多地用于本地运行模型，该项目正处于这一趋势之中。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://calif.io/java4shell/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Java4Shell: RCE in OpenJDK when processing Base64</span><span class='zh'>Java4Shell：处理 Base64 时 OpenJDK 中的远程代码执行漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">calif.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Calif disclosed Java4Shell, tracked as CVE-2026-114938, a memory-corruption vulnerability in OpenJDK&#x27;s Base64 decoder.</span><span class='zh'>Calif 披露了 Java4Shell（编号 CVE-2026-114938），这是 OpenJDK Base64 解码器中的内存破坏漏洞。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Calif disclosed Java4Shell, tracked as CVE-2026-114938, a memory-corruption vulnerability in OpenJDK&#x27;s Base64 decoder. OpenJDK is described as the most popular Java runtime. The vulnerability has a CVSS 4.0 score of 9.2 out of 10, rated Critical. Its severity could make affected OpenJDK deployments a high-priority patching target.</span><span class='zh'>Calif 披露了 Java4Shell（编号 CVE-2026-114938），这是 OpenJDK Base64 解码器中的内存破坏漏洞。OpenJDK 被称为最流行的 Java 运行时。该漏洞在 CVSS 4.0 中获得 9.2 分（满分 10 分），评级为“严重”。这一评级意味着受影响的 OpenJDK 部署可能需要优先修补。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://macanorak.com/the-macbook-pros-oled-pwm-problem-when-a-better-display-becomes-worse-to-use/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The MacBook Pro&#x27;s OLED PWM problem</span><span class='zh'>MacBook Pro 的 OLED PWM 问题：更好的显示屏为何更难用</span></a><span class="dom">macanorak.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple has introduced tandem OLED technology to the MacBook Pro, alongside the Mini-LED option that remains available.</span><span class='zh'>苹果为 MacBook Pro 引入了串联 OLED 技术，同时继续提供 Mini-LED 版本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple has introduced tandem OLED technology to the MacBook Pro, alongside the Mini-LED option that remains available. The display offers individually illuminated pixels, effectively infinite contrast, deeper blacks, higher peak HDR brightness, and touch input, prompting an overwhelmingly positive initial reaction. The source argues that impressive image quality does not necessarily make the screen comfortable for every user, particularly because of its PWM behavior.</span><span class='zh'>苹果为 MacBook Pro 引入了串联 OLED 技术，同时继续提供 Mini-LED 版本。该显示屏具备独立发光像素、近乎无限的对比度、更深的黑色、更高的 HDR 峰值亮度，并首次支持触控，发布初期反响普遍积极。文章指出，出色的画质并不一定意味着所有用户都能舒适使用，尤其是其 PWM 特性可能带来问题。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Starlink Mini 2 is presented as providing fast, reliable internet both at home and on the go.</span><span class='zh'>Starlink Mini 2 被定位为可在家中和移动途中提供快速、可靠的互联网连接。</span></p>

The service is intended to support streaming, video calls, gaming, and other high-speed connectivity needs, including in rural and remote locations.</span><span class='zh'>Starlink Mini 2 被定位为可在家中和移动途中提供快速、可靠的互联网连接。该服务支持流媒体、视频通话、游戏等高速联网需求，也面向乡村和偏远地区。</span></p>

The notice identifies the affected services but provides no further incident details in the available information.</span><span class='zh'>Claude 状态页报告称，claude.ai、Claude Code 和 platform.claude.com 的错误率升高。现有信息仅列出了受影响的服务，未提供更多事故细节。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://litnews.ai/articles/garciaguirre-samsung-sk-hynix-micron-dram-conspiracy-suit-survives-dismissal" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Court denies motion to dismiss DRAM price fixing case</span><span class='zh'>法院驳回 DRAM 价格操纵案的撤诉申请</span></a><span class="dom">litnews.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A federal judge refused to dismiss a proposed class action accusing Samsung, SK Hynix, and Micron of conspiring to restrict conventional DRAM supply and shift capacity toward HBM.</span><span class='zh'>一名联邦法官拒绝驳回一项拟议集体诉讼，该诉讼指控三星、SK 海力士和美光合谋限制传统 DRAM 供应，并将产能转向 HBM。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A federal judge refused to dismiss a proposed class action accusing Samsung, SK Hynix, and Micron of conspiring to restrict conventional DRAM supply and shift capacity toward HBM. Judge Noël Wise found that buyers had plausibly alleged an agreement violating Section 1 of the Sherman Act. The allegations have been accepted only for this procedural stage and have not been proven.</span><span class='zh'>一名联邦法官拒绝驳回一项拟议集体诉讼，该诉讼指控三星、SK 海力士和美光合谋限制传统 DRAM 供应，并将产能转向 HBM。Noël Wise 法官认定，买方对违反《谢尔曼法》第 1 条的合谋提出了具有可信性的指控。目前这些指控仅在这一程序阶段被视为成立，并未得到证实。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/15/top_stories_11152026'><span class='en'>Nov 15</span><span class='zh'>11月15日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>492</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50502988' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>219</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50443824" data-id="50443824" data-tags="Legal" data-score="481" data-comments="267" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50443824" data-time="1794333722">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lwn.net/SubscriberLink/1102549/acbc92e00cee3a3f/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Synaptics patent claims stop work on DisplayLink drivers</span><span class='zh'>Synaptics 的专利主张叫停 DisplayLink 驱动开发</span></a><span class="dom">lwn.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mike Lothian tried to improve a Linux driver for a DisplayLink docking station after finding its performance inadequate.</span><span class='zh'>Mike Lothian 认为 DisplayLink 扩展坞在 Linux 下性能不佳，因此尝试改进其驱动。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mike Lothian tried to improve a Linux driver for a DisplayLink docking station after finding its performance inadequate. Synaptics, which acquired DisplayLink in 2022, responded with patent-infringement claims, a demand to stop the unauthorized implementation, and warnings of costly legal penalties. Linux users of the company’s docks therefore appear likely to remain without feature parity for now.</span><span class='zh'>Mike Lothian 认为 DisplayLink 扩展坞在 Linux 下性能不佳，因此尝试改进其驱动。DisplayLink 于 2022 年被 Synaptics 收购，而 Synaptics 随后以专利侵权为由要求停止未经授权的实现，并警告可能面临高昂法律处罚。因此，相关扩展坞的 Linux 用户目前似乎仍将无法获得与其他平台相当的功能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.cnn.com/2026/11/17/politics/highly-classified-national-security-communications-network-goes-down" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Highly classified US national security communications network goes down</span><span class='zh'>高度机密的美国国家安全通信网络发生故障</span></a><span class="dom">cnn.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The highly classified network used by the National Security Agency for internal communications is down, according to six people familiar with the matter.</span><span class='zh'>据六名知情人士透露，美国国家安全局用于内部通信的高度机密网络目前发生故障。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The highly classified network used by the National Security Agency for internal communications is down, according to six people familiar with the matter. The reported outage affects a communications system supporting the agency’s internal operations.</span><span class='zh'>据六名知情人士透露，美国国家安全局用于内部通信的高度机密网络目前发生故障。此次中断影响了支持该机构内部运作的通信系统。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/17/top_stories_11172026'><span class='en'>Nov 17</span><span class='zh'>11月17日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>5.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>476</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50532849' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>551</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50496602" data-id="50496602" data-tags="Privacy|AI" data-score="468" data-comments="175" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50496602" data-time="1794660345">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/11/tech-companies-are-trying-shift-entire-concept-privacy-again" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/mobile-privacy.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='eff.org' data-c='#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">54</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/11/tech-companies-are-trying-shift-entire-concept-privacy-again" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tech companies are trying to shift the concept of privacy again</span><span class='zh'>科技公司又在试图改变隐私的定义</span></a><span class="dom">eff.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Privacy is increasingly affecting people beyond the individual who owns or uses a device.</span><span class='zh'>隐私问题正越来越多地影响设备所有者或使用者之外的人。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Privacy is increasingly affecting people beyond the individual who owns or uses a device. Meta and Google’s smart glasses can record what the wearer is looking at or use AI to describe it, extending the consequences of data collection to bystanders. The shift challenges the older framing of privacy as primarily a matter of personal choice.</span><span class='zh'>隐私问题正越来越多地影响设备所有者或使用者之外的人。Meta 和 Google 的智能眼镜可以记录佩戴者所看到的内容，或使用 AI 对其进行描述，使数据收集的后果波及旁观者。这种变化挑战了过去主要将隐私视为个人选择的传统定义。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/14/top_stories_11142026'><span class='en'>Nov 14</span><span class='zh'>11月14日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>3.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>468</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50496602' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>175</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50468888" data-id="50468888" data-tags="AI|Hardware|Code" data-score="466" data-comments="180" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50468888" data-time="1794496990">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/hyperionaidev/smolmaxxing/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">55</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/hyperionaidev/smolmaxxing/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I&#x27;m making LLMs work on small and old devices</span><span class='zh'>让大语言模型运行在小型和老旧设备上</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>smolmaxxing explores how to run the largest practical language models on small or old devices such as TV boxes, phones, single-board computers, and routers.</span><span class='zh'>smolmaxxing 探索如何在电视盒子、旧手机、单板计算机和路由器等小型或老旧设备上运行实际可用的最大语言模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>smolmaxxing explores how to run the largest practical language models on small or old devices such as TV boxes, phones, single-board computers, and routers. It uses custom kernels, memory optimization, and weight streaming, with claims measured on the actual hardware. On a Xiaomi TV Box S 3rd Gen with about 1 GB of usable RAM, the project reports running models ranging from 260K to 16B parameters.</span><span class='zh'>smolmaxxing 探索如何在电视盒子、旧手机、单板计算机和路由器等小型或老旧设备上运行实际可用的最大语言模型。项目使用定制内核、内存优化和权重流式传输，并在真实硬件上测量各项结果。在可用内存约 1 GB 的 Xiaomi TV Box S 第三代上，项目报告称可运行参数量从 26 万到 160 亿不等的模型。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>466</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50468888' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>180</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50493904" data-id="50493904" data-tags="Gaming" data-score="466" data-comments="147" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50493904" data-time="1794630210">
<a class="thumb" href="https://monkeyisland.pages.dev/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://monkeyisland.pages.dev/og-image.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='monkeyisland.pages.dev' data-c='#a855f7' data-tag='Gaming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">56</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://monkeyisland.pages.dev/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Monkey Island: all 13 versions side by side</span><span class='zh'>《猴岛的秘密》全部 13 个版本并排对比</span></a><span class="dom">monkeyisland.pages.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project presents every version of The Secret of Monkey Island from 1990 side by side, covering eight editions and 13 display modes across systems including Hercules, CGA, EGA, VGA, Amiga, Atari ST, Macintosh, FM Towns, and Sega CD.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目并排展示了 1990 年《猴岛的秘密》的所有版本，涵盖 Hercules、CGA、EGA、VGA、Amiga、Atari ST、Macintosh、FM Towns 和 Sega CD 等平台上的 8 个版本、13 种显示模式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project presents every version of The Secret of Monkey Island from 1990 side by side, covering eight editions and 13 display modes across systems including Hercules, CGA, EGA, VGA, Amiga, Atari ST, Macintosh, FM Towns, and Sega CD. It shows the same 12 scenes in each version, with captures scaled to 1600×1200 using nearest-neighbor scaling and corrected for a 4:3 display. The presentation keeps the framing constant so differences come from the original machines and display modes.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目并排展示了 1990 年《猴岛的秘密》的所有版本，涵盖 Hercules、CGA、EGA、VGA、Amiga、Atari ST、Macintosh、FM Towns 和 Sega CD 等平台上的 8 个版本、13 种显示模式。每个版本都展示相同的 12 个场景，截图使用最近邻算法放大到 1600×1200，并进行 4:3 显示比例校正。展示保持相同构图，因此差异主要来自原始机器和显示模式。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Gaming' style='--c:#a855f7'>Gaming</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/14/top_stories_11142026'><span class='en'>Nov 14</span><span class='zh'>11月14日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>466</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50493904' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>147</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50506180" data-id="50506180" data-tags="Politics|Business|Hardware" data-score="463" data-comments="207" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50506180" data-time="1794742851">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/MSFT365Status/status/2121079061774491735" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microsoft Outlook/365 still down after 12 hours</span><span class='zh'>Microsoft Outlook/365 持续中断 12 小时后仍未恢复</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft said changes intended to improve serviceability were not producing the expected results during the Outlook/365 outage.</span><span class='zh'>微软表示，在 Outlook/365 宕机期间，为改善服务能力而实施的变更没有达到预期效果。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft said changes intended to improve serviceability were not producing the expected results during the Outlook/365 outage. The company was identifying alternative workstreams to increase capacity amid sustained traffic-management imbalances. The service had remained disrupted for more than 12 hours.</span><span class='zh'>微软表示，在 Outlook/365 宕机期间，为改善服务能力而实施的变更没有达到预期效果。面对持续的流量管理失衡，公司正在寻找其他工作方案以提升容量。服务中断当时已持续超过 12 个小时。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://steveblank.com/2026/11/15/whats-next-for-nvidia/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What&#x27;s Next for Nvidia?</span><span class='zh'>英伟达的下一步是什么？</span></a><span class="dom">steveblank.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Nvidia’s board announced that co-founder Jensen Huang had resigned after 33 years at the company.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达董事会宣布，联合创始人黄仁勋在公司任职 33 年后辞职。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nvidia’s board announced that co-founder Jensen Huang had resigned after 33 years at the company. The change raises questions about what happens when a company loses a visionary chief executive. The supplied discussion argues that Nvidia could risk losing innovation and relying on momentum and its brand.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达董事会宣布，联合创始人黄仁勋在公司任职 33 年后辞职。这一变动引发了人们对公司失去具有远见的首席执行官后将如何发展的疑问。相关讨论认为，英伟达可能面临创新减弱、转而依赖既有势头和品牌的风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ductus.sorenblank.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Handwriting, as a Font</span><span class='zh'>把手写字迹变成字体</span></a><span class="dom">ductus.sorenblank.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This browser tool turns a user’s handwriting into a font.</span><span class='zh'>这个浏览器工具可以将用户的手写字迹转换为字体。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This browser tool turns a user’s handwriting into a font. Users write each letter in the browser and download the result as OTF or WOFF2. No account or upload is required, and the letters remain on the device.</span><span class='zh'>这个浏览器工具可以将用户的手写字迹转换为字体。用户在浏览器中书写每个字母，然后下载 OTF 或 WOFF2 格式的字体。它无需账户或上传，字母会保留在设备上。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://bruceroettgers.eu/blog/protomaps/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Protomaps: Self-hosting map tiles has gotten cheap and easy</span><span class='zh'>Protomaps：自托管地图瓦片如今既便宜又简单</span></a><span class="dom">bruceroettgers.eu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Protomaps uses PMTiles, a tile format that stores a map in a single file and serves it through HTTP Range requests.</span><span class='zh'>Protomaps 使用 PMTiles，这种瓦片格式将地图存储在单个文件中，并通过 HTTP Range 请求提供服务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Protomaps uses PMTiles, a tile format that stores a map in a single file and serves it through HTTP Range requests. The file can be uploaded to a storage bucket and cached by compatible CDNs, while an edge function can translate conventional x/y/z tile URLs for services such as Cloudflare. This makes map data portable and inexpensive to deploy compared with usage-priced commercial APIs.</span><span class='zh'>Protomaps 使用 PMTiles，这种瓦片格式将地图存储在单个文件中，并通过 HTTP Range 请求提供服务。该文件可以上传到存储桶并由支持相关缓存的 CDN 缓存；对于 Cloudflare 等服务，也可以用边缘函数将传统的 x/y/z 瓦片 URL 转换为 Range 请求。与按使用量收费的商业 API 相比，这让地图数据更易移植，也更便宜易部署。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/15/top_stories_11152026'><span class='en'>Nov 15</span><span class='zh'>11月15日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>455</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50509837' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>83</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50473190" data-id="50473190" data-tags="Business" data-score="448" data-comments="477" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50473190" data-time="1794513093">

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.vulncheck.com/blog/anthropic-glasswing-november-update" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.vulncheck.com/_og/s/o_6eba28d38c5a4083.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='vulncheck.com' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.vulncheck.com/blog/anthropic-glasswing-november-update" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tracking Anthropic-discovered vulnerabilities</span><span class='zh'>追踪 Anthropic 发现的漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">vulncheck.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Seven months into Project Glasswing, VulnCheck says it has tracked 442 CVEs attributed to Anthropic, representing about 0.5% of all CVEs published that year.</span><span class='zh'>在 Project Glasswing 启动七个月后，VulnCheck 表示已追踪到 442 个归因于 Anthropic 的 CVE，约占当年发布的全部 CVE 的 0.5%。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Seven months into Project Glasswing, VulnCheck says it has tracked 442 CVEs attributed to Anthropic, representing about 0.5% of all CVEs published that year. Only two were identified as exploited in the wild, while 7,349 of Anthropic&#x27;s 37,966 reported findings had been made public and 217 were marked fixed. The project began with Anthropic&#x27;s claim that it had found thousands of additional high- and critical-severity vulnerabilities in open-source software.</span><span class='zh'>在 Project Glasswing 启动七个月后，VulnCheck 表示已追踪到 442 个归因于 Anthropic 的 CVE，约占当年发布的全部 CVE 的 0.5%。其中只有两个被确认已在现实环境中遭到利用；Anthropic 报告的 37,966 项发现中，有 7,349 项已公开，217 项被标记为已修复。该项目源于 Anthropic 的说法：其发现了数千个开源软件中的新增高危和严重漏洞。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/10/top_stories_11102026'><span class='en'>Nov 10</span><span class='zh'>11月10日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>447</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50447074' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>219</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50517321" data-id="50517321" data-tags="Business" data-score="438" data-comments="334" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50517321" data-time="1794835102">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theregister.com/saas/2026/11/16/microsoft-blames-monster-email-outage-on-software-defect-introduced-in-july-fix/5310026" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microsoft blames monster email outage on software defect introduced in July fix</span><span class='zh'>微软称大规模电子邮件故障源于 7 月修复引入的软件缺陷</span></a><span class="dom">theregister.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft blamed last week’s major Microsoft 365 email outage on a defect introduced by a July 2026 change.</span><span class='zh'>微软将上周 Microsoft 365 大规模电子邮件故障归因于 2026 年 7 月一次变更引入的软件缺陷。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft blamed last week’s major Microsoft 365 email outage on a defect introduced by a July 2026 change. The defect remained dormant in network gateway servers until increased load and reduced capacity triggered it, causing a day-long disruption. Microsoft’s preliminary post-incident report said the change had passed load testing and initially caused no impact after deployment.</span><span class='zh'>微软将上周 Microsoft 365 大规模电子邮件故障归因于 2026 年 7 月一次变更引入的软件缺陷。该缺陷一直潜伏在网络网关服务器中，直到负载上升、容量下降时被触发，造成持续一天的中断。微软的初步事后报告称，该变更通过了负载测试，部署后最初也没有造成影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://unemploymentlabs.com/checkpoint-forensics" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Chinese frontier AI models carry the fingerprint of export-controlled GPUs</span><span class='zh'>中国前沿 AI 模型带有受出口管制 GPU 的指纹</span></a><span class="dom">unemploymentlabs.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The material points to an analysis claiming that Chinese frontier AI models carry identifiable characteristics of export-controlled GPUs.</span><span class='zh'>现有材料指向一项分析，该分析声称中国前沿 AI 模型带有受出口管制 GPU 的可识别特征。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The material points to an analysis claiming that Chinese frontier AI models carry identifiable characteristics of export-controlled GPUs. No further methodological details or conclusions are available here.</span><span class='zh'>现有材料指向一项分析，该分析声称中国前沿 AI 模型带有受出口管制 GPU 的可识别特征。现有信息未提供更多方法或结论细节。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://burntsushi.net/jiff/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Jiff 1.0: A datetime library for Rust</span><span class='zh'>Jiff 1.0：Rust 的日期时间库</span></a><span class="dom">burntsushi.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Jiff 1.0 is a datetime library for Rust that puts time-zone handling at the center of its API.</span><span class='zh'>Jiff 1.0 是一个 Rust 日期时间库，其 API 将时区处理置于核心位置。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Jiff 1.0 is a datetime library for Rust that puts time-zone handling at the center of its API. It automatically addresses many common daylight-saving-time problems and is already widely adopted in the Rust ecosystem. The name is pronounced like “gif,” with a soft “g,” as in “gem.”</span><span class='zh'>Jiff 1.0 是一个 Rust 日期时间库，其 API 将时区处理置于核心位置。它会自动处理许多与夏令时有关的常见问题，并已在 Rust 生态中得到广泛采用。Jiff 的发音类似带软 g 音的“gif”，就像“gem”中的 g。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://av.github.io/safe-sand/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hyper-realistic sand with WebGL2</span><span class='zh'>使用 WebGL2 实现超逼真的沙子模拟</span></a><span class="dom">av.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This browser-based sand simulator uses WebGL2 and JavaScript to model moving grains.</span><span class='zh'>这款浏览器沙子模拟器使用 WebGL2 和 JavaScript 模拟沙粒运动。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This browser-based sand simulator uses WebGL2 and JavaScript to model moving grains. Users can draw and push the sand, tilt a phone to slide the grains, or shake the device to scatter them. It supports touch and motion controls on phones and tablets, as well as desktop browsers.</span><span class='zh'>这款浏览器沙子模拟器使用 WebGL2 和 JavaScript 模拟沙粒运动。用户可以绘制并推动沙子，在手机上倾斜设备让沙粒滑动，或摇晃设备将其散开。它支持手机和平板上的触控与动作控制，也可在桌面浏览器中运行。</span></p>
