<a class="thumb" href="https://www.theguardian.com/science/2026/nov/13/kurt-godel-notebooks-ai-helps-unlock-hidden-discoveries-man-who-transformed-maths" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/6eeccf216579fe2181ae737d30f31abd7590125c/210_58_1759_1407/master/1759.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=334efc63d7472a97d72be0ffd23af8bb' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='theguardian.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">70</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theguardian.com/science/2026/nov/13/kurt-godel-notebooks-ai-helps-unlock-hidden-discoveries-man-who-transformed-maths" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI unlocks Gödel&#x27;s notebooks, said to contain hundreds of proved theorems</span><span class='zh'>AI 解读哥德尔笔记本，据称其中包含数百个已证明定理</span></a><span class="dom">theguardian.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kurt Gödel left 1,009 pages of densely written material, much of it in an obsolete shorthand, and the notebooks are said to contain hundreds of proved theorems.</span><span class='zh'>库尔特·哥德尔留下了 1,009 页密集书写的材料，其中许多使用已过时的速记，据称笔记本里包含数百个已证明定理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kurt Gödel left 1,009 pages of densely written material, much of it in an obsolete shorthand, and the notebooks are said to contain hundreds of proved theorems. Gödel’s published incompleteness theorems transformed mathematics and helped pave the way for the digital age. His unpublished work spans tens of thousands of pages and remains of interest to historians of science.</span><span class='zh'>库尔特·哥德尔留下了 1,009 页密集书写的材料，其中许多使用已过时的速记，据称笔记本里包含数百个已证明定理。哥德尔发表的不完备性定理改变了数学，并为数字时代铺平了道路。他未发表的作品多达数万页，至今仍受到科学史学者关注。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/13/top_stories_11132026'><span class='en'>Nov 13</span><span class='zh'>11月13日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>424</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50486748' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>107</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50443812" data-id="50443812" data-tags="Show HN|Web|Programming|Open Source|Code" data-score="423" data-comments="141" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50443812" data-time="1794333675">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/GoogleChrome/modern-web-guidance" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">71</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/GoogleChrome/modern-web-guidance" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Get your coding agent off ~2019 JavaScript, onto modern web primitives</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：让编码代理摆脱约 2019 年的 JavaScript，转向现代 Web 原语</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Chrome-led project provides coding agents with curated guidance on modern, native web APIs.</span><span class='zh'>一个由 Chrome 发起的项目为编码代理提供现代原生 Web API 的精选指南。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Chrome-led project provides coding agents with curated guidance on modern, native web APIs. Examples include CSS highlights, dialog and showModal(), and field-sizing, which can replace common JavaScript-heavy patterns. The open-source release contains more than 200 expert-written guides, developed with Firefox, MDN, and Edge, and is designed for agent skills and retrieval-augmented tooling.</span><span class='zh'>一个由 Chrome 发起的项目为编码代理提供现代原生 Web API 的精选指南。例如，CSS Highlights、dialog 与 showModal()、field-sizing 都可以替代常见的重 JavaScript 实现。该开源项目目前包含 200 多份由专家编写的指南，并与 Firefox、MDN 和 Edge 合作开发，面向代理技能和检索增强工具。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/10/top_stories_11102026'><span class='en'>Nov 10</span><span class='zh'>11月10日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>423</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50443812' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>141</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50443427" data-id="50443427" data-tags="Startups" data-score="418" data-comments="100" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50443427" data-time="1794331874">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">72</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.val.town/winding-down" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Val Town is winding down</span><span class='zh'>Val Town 即将停止运营</span></a><span class="dom">blog.val.town</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Val Town has decided to wind down its service.</span><span class='zh'>Val Town 决定逐步停止运营。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Val Town has decided to wind down its service. The team thanked users for trusting the platform and apologized that it could no longer continue serving them. The announcement does not provide further details about the shutdown timeline or transition.</span><span class='zh'>Val Town 决定逐步停止运营。团队感谢用户对平台的信任，并为无法继续提供服务表示歉意。公告没有进一步说明停运时间表或迁移安排。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.joshka.net/cell-borders/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Terminal borders that don&#x27;t cost a cell</span><span class='zh'>不占用终端单元格的边框</span></a><span class="dom">joshka.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A proposed terminal protocol draws thin borders, rounded corners, and gridlines along cell edges without consuming rows or columns.</span><span class='zh'>一种新的终端协议可以沿单元格边缘绘制细线、圆角和网格线，而不占用额外的行或列。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A proposed terminal protocol draws thin borders, rounded corners, and gridlines along cell edges without consuming rows or columns. The implementation works in Ghostty, with demos built using Ratatui. It aims to give terminal user interfaces more visual design options than conventional box-based layouts.</span><span class='zh'>一种新的终端协议可以沿单元格边缘绘制细线、圆角和网格线，而不占用额外的行或列。该实现可在 Ghostty 中运行，并提供了使用 Ratatui 构建的演示。它旨在为终端用户界面提供比传统方框布局更多的视觉设计选择。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A website owner noticed a new backlink from w2rt6byl5nf1opmc-a.com while demonstrating a backlink monitor to a friend.</span><span class='zh'>一位网站所有者在向朋友演示反向链接监控器时，发现 w2rt6byl5nf1opmc-a.com 突然产生了一个新链接。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml/releases/tag/v1.0.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml/releases/tag/v1.0.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ggml v1.0.0</span><span class='zh'>ggml v1.0.0</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ggml 1.0.0 removes deprecated public APIs and the legacy DIAG_MASK operations, while adding configurable YaRN truncation and FP8 support.</span><span class='zh'>ggml 1.0.0 移除了已弃用的公共 API 和旧版 DIAG_MASK 操作，并加入可配置的 YaRN 截断和 FP8 支持。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ggml 1.0.0 removes deprecated public APIs and the legacy DIAG_MASK operations, while adding configurable YaRN truncation and FP8 support. It also delivers performance work across CUDA, Metal, Vulkan, SYCL, OpenCL, WebGPU, and Hexagon backends, including quantized matrix multiplication, MoE fusion, FlashAttention paths, and scheduler improvements. The release broadens hardware and backend support while reducing unnecessary input uploads.</span><span class='zh'>ggml 1.0.0 移除了已弃用的公共 API 和旧版 DIAG_MASK 操作，并加入可配置的 YaRN 截断和 FP8 支持。该版本还改进了 CUDA、Metal、Vulkan、SYCL、OpenCL、WebGPU 和 Hexagon 后端的性能，包括量化矩阵乘法、MoE 融合、FlashAttention 路径和调度器优化。此次发布扩大了硬件与后端支持，并减少了不必要的输入上传。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">78</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.smithsonianmag.com/smart-news/astronomers-found-a-mysterious-object-orbiting-far-beyond-pluto-it-could-be-a-new-dwarf-planet-hidden-in-our-solar-system-180989857/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Dwarf planet candidate appears which takes roughly 280 years to orbit our sun</span><span class='zh'>一颗矮行星候选体出现了，绕太阳一周约需 280 年</span></a><span class="dom">smithsonianmag.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Astronomers identified a distant object called ED138 in telescope images from the Vera C.</span><span class='zh'>天文学家在维拉·鲁宾天文台的望远镜图像中发现了一个名为 ED138 的遥远天体。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Astronomers identified a distant object called ED138 in telescope images from the Vera C. Rubin Observatory. It appears unusually large and would take roughly 280 years to orbit the Sun. Its size still needs to be determined before astronomers can classify it as a dwarf planet.</span><span class='zh'>天文学家在维拉·鲁宾天文台的望远镜图像中发现了一个名为 ED138 的遥远天体。它看起来异常大，绕太阳一周大约需要 280 年。在将其归类为矮行星之前，天文学家仍需确定它的大小。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://gatezh.com/posts/macos-secure-enclave-ssh-keys/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://gatezh.com/brand/share.381cc582d58a3410686473b92c1d944fbc83dc59a7e926952aeb6c5c0d8a6ef0.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='gatezh.com' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">79</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gatezh.com/posts/macos-secure-enclave-ssh-keys/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Protecting SSH Keys with macOS Secure Enclave</span><span class='zh'>使用 macOS 安全隔区保护 SSH 密钥</span></a><span class="dom">gatezh.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The proposed setup stores SSH keys in the macOS Secure Enclave and requires Touch ID for each use, without third-party applications.</span><span class='zh'>这种方案将 SSH 密钥存储在 macOS 安全隔区中，并在每次使用时要求通过 Touch ID 验证，无需第三方应用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The proposed setup stores SSH keys in the macOS Secure Enclave and requires Touch ID for each use, without third-party applications. It is intended to prevent malicious software that can read the filesystem from simply copying private keys from ~/.ssh/. The approach is motivated in part by the risk of running untrusted code from fake interview assignments.</span><span class='zh'>这种方案将 SSH 密钥存储在 macOS 安全隔区中，并在每次使用时要求通过 Touch ID 验证，无需第三方应用。它旨在防止能够读取文件系统的恶意软件直接复制 ~/.ssh/ 中的私钥。作者提到，虚假面试作业要求用户克隆并运行代码的案例，也是采用这一方案的原因之一。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>382</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50449748' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>136</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50487864" data-id="50487864" data-tags="Hardware|Business" data-score="382" data-comments="211" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50487864" data-time="1794595244">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.freightwaves.com/news/11-4-million-in-nvidia-hardware-vanishes-during-shipment-from-california-to-texas" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.freightwaves.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/11/09/NVIDIA-Endeavor-1200x628-1.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='freightwaves.com' data-c='#f59e0b' data-tag='Hardware'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">80</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.freightwaves.com/news/11-4-million-in-nvidia-hardware-vanishes-during-shipment-from-california-to-texas" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>$11.4M in Nvidia hardware vanishes during shipment from CA to TX</span><span class='zh'>价值 1,140 万美元的英伟达硬件在从加州运往德州途中失踪</span></a><span class="dom">freightwaves.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Six pallets of NVIDIA computer hardware, reportedly worth $11.4 million, disappeared during shipment from Oakland, California, to Fort Worth, Texas.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，六托盘英伟达计算机硬件在从加州奥克兰运往德州沃思堡途中失踪，价值 1,140 万美元。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Six pallets of NVIDIA computer hardware, reportedly worth $11.4 million, disappeared during shipment from Oakland, California, to Fort Worth, Texas. The truck was collected on August 29, made several extended stops, and was last tracked in San Bernardino County. Oakland police confirmed an ongoing grand-theft investigation, while the carrier’s identity and the circumstances remain unclear.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，六托盘英伟达计算机硬件在从加州奥克兰运往德州沃思堡途中失踪，价值 1,140 万美元。卡车于 8 月 29 日提货，途中多次长时间停留，最后在圣贝纳迪诺县失去踪迹。奥克兰警方已确认正在调查这起重大盗窃案，但承运方身份和货物失踪经过仍不清楚。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ransomnews.com/burger-king-tweet-bitly-link-hijack-2026/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Old Bitly links are being hijacked</span><span class='zh'>旧 Bitly 链接正遭劫持</span></a><span class="dom">ransomnews.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Burger King and McDonald’s were not hacked.</span><span class='zh'>汉堡王和麦当劳并未遭到黑客入侵。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Burger King and McDonald’s were not hacked. Instead, someone re-registered Bitly links posted in their 2011 tweets. The same person or group has replaced 57 of 97 links in old tweets by Donald Trump.</span><span class='zh'>汉堡王和麦当劳并未遭到黑客入侵。有人重新注册了它们在 2011 年推文中使用的 Bitly 链接。同样的问题还影响了唐纳德·特朗普旧推文中的 97 个链接，其中 57 个已被替换。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/15/top_stories_11152026'><span class='en'>Nov 15</span><span class='zh'>11月15日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>374</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50511395' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>196</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50463899" data-id="50463899" data-tags="Security" data-score="370" data-comments="220" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50463899" data-time="1794466368">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://forums.spacebattles.com/threads/humble-bundle-just-completely-killed-most-of-their-security-through-vibecoding.1346295/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>&quot;Humble Bundle just killed most of their security through vibecoding&quot;</span><span class='zh'>“Humble Bundle 的大部分安全性都被氛围编程干掉了”</span></a><span class="dom">forums.spacebattles.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The discussion claims that Humble Bundle substantially weakened its security through “vibecoding.” The available description does not identify the affected systems, specific vulnerabilities, or the evidence behind the claim.</span><span class='zh'>这场讨论声称，Humble Bundle 通过“氛围编程”大幅削弱了自身的大部分安全性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The discussion claims that Humble Bundle substantially weakened its security through “vibecoding.” The available description does not identify the affected systems, specific vulnerabilities, or the evidence behind the claim.</span><span class='zh'>这场讨论声称，Humble Bundle 通过“氛围编程”大幅削弱了自身的大部分安全性。现有描述没有说明受影响的系统、具体漏洞或支持这一说法的证据。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://codeberg.org/wvhulle/bootvine" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A live tree of systemd jobs on the boot console</span><span class='zh'>启动控制台上的 systemd 作业实时树</span></a><span class="dom">codeberg.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>bootvine is a boot animation that displays systemd stages as a tree.</span><span class='zh'>bootvine 是一款启动动画，会将 systemd 的各个阶段显示为一棵树。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>bootvine is a boot animation that displays systemd stages as a tree. It provides a visual representation of systemd jobs during system startup.</span><span class='zh'>bootvine 是一款启动动画，会将 systemd 的各个阶段显示为一棵树。它在系统启动过程中以可视化方式呈现 systemd 作业。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span><span class='tag' data-t='Startups' style='--c:#fb7185'>Startups</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>360</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50425017' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>145</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50465352" data-id="50465352" data-tags="Science|Programming" data-score="358" data-comments="169" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50465352" data-time="1794479306">
<a class="thumb" href="https://pron.github.io/posts/correctness-and-complexity" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#10b981' data-tag='Science'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=pron.github.io&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>pron.github.io</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">84</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://pron.github.io/posts/correctness-and-complexity" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Writing correct software is hard and math alone won’t help us (2016)</span><span class='zh'>写出正确的软件很难，光靠数学也无济于事（2016）</span></a><span class="dom">pron.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The essay examines why writing correct programs is inherently difficult.</span><span class='zh'>这篇文章探讨了为什么编写正确的程序本质上很困难。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The essay examines why writing correct programs is inherently difficult. It reviews results from computability and complexity theory, arguing that complete understanding of a program—needed to establish correctness—is fundamentally at odds with the nature of programs. The piece expands a Curry On talk on computational complexity and software verification, with additional proofs and results.</span><span class='zh'>这篇文章探讨了为什么编写正确的程序本质上很困难。文章回顾可计算性理论和复杂性理论的成果，认为要证明正确性所需的完整理解，与程序本身的性质存在根本冲突。文章扩展了作者在 Curry On 大会关于计算复杂性和软件验证的演讲，并加入了更多证明和结果。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>358</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50465352' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>169</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50478515" data-id="50478515" data-tags="Open Source|Programming" data-score="356" data-comments="217" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50478515" data-time="1794545693">
<a class="thumb" href="https://ice-wm.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=ice-wm.org&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>ice-wm.org</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">85</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ice-wm.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>IceWM</span><span class='zh'>IceWM</span></a><span class="dom">ice-wm.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>IceWM is a fast, simple, and highly configurable window manager for the X Window System.</span><span class='zh'>IceWM 是面向 X Window System 的快速、简洁且高度可配置的窗口管理器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>IceWM is a fast, simple, and highly configurable window manager for the X Window System. It provides a taskbar, keyboard and mouse controls, dynamic menus, multiple-monitor support through RandR and Xinerama, themes, and optional session and wallpaper managers. Created by Marko Maček in 1997 using C++, it is now maintained on GitHub and is available on many Linux distributions and BSD systems.</span><span class='zh'>IceWM 是面向 X Window System 的快速、简洁且高度可配置的窗口管理器。它提供任务栏、键盘和鼠标控制、动态菜单，以及通过 RandR 和 Xinerama 实现的多显示器支持，还支持主题和可选的会话、壁纸管理器。IceWM 由 Marko Maček 于 1997 年使用 C++ 创建，目前在 GitHub 上维护，并可用于多种 Linux 发行版和 BSD 系统。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/13/top_stories_11132026'><span class='en'>Nov 13</span><span class='zh'>11月13日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>356</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50478515' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>217</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50462177" data-id="50462177" data-tags="Security" data-score="355" data-comments="191" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50462177" data-time="1794449471">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.wiz.io/blog/cve-2026-119618-rce-apache-http-server-vulnerability" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.datocms-assets.com/75231/1794425853-threat-update-kics-alert_custom.jpeg?fm=webp' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='wiz.io' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">86</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.wiz.io/blog/cve-2026-119618-rce-apache-http-server-vulnerability" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Critical RCE Vulnerability in Apache HTTP Server</span><span class='zh'>Apache HTTP Server 存在严重远程代码执行漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">wiz.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>CVE-2026-119618 is a critical race-condition vulnerability in Apache HTTP Server’s HTTP/2 module, mod_http2.</span><span class='zh'>CVE-2026-119618 是 Apache HTTP Server HTTP/2 模块 mod_http2 中一个严重的竞态条件漏洞。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>CVE-2026-119618 is a critical race-condition vulnerability in Apache HTTP Server’s HTTP/2 module, mod_http2. The flaw affects the assign_headers() function when it handles shared resources, and a remote unauthenticated attacker could use it to corrupt heap memory and execute arbitrary code. Wiz reports that 64% of cloud environments have vulnerable resources; no public exploit or in-the-wild exploitation had been reported at publication time.</span><span class='zh'>CVE-2026-119618 是 Apache HTTP Server HTTP/2 模块 mod_http2 中一个严重的竞态条件漏洞。该漏洞出现在 assign_headers() 函数处理共享资源时，未经身份验证的远程攻击者可能借此破坏堆内存并执行任意代码。Wiz 报告称，64% 的云环境存在易受攻击的资源；截至发布时，尚无公开漏洞利用程序或野外攻击报告。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>355</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50462177' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>191</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50532086" data-id="50532086" data-tags="Open Source|AI|Programming|DevOps|Energy" data-score="344" data-comments="234" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50532086" data-time="1794926736">
<a class="thumb" href="https://jordankrueger.com/blog/better-open-source-not-more-apps/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://jordankrueger.com/images/gallery/blog-better-open-source-not-more-apps.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='jordankrueger.com' data-c='#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">87</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://jordankrueger.com/blog/better-open-source-not-more-apps/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We don&#x27;t need more apps, we need better open source</span><span class='zh'>我们不需要更多应用，而需要更好的开源软件</span></a><span class="dom">jordankrueger.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI has made it easy to build small applications over a weekend, such as habit trackers, to-do lists, and note-taking tools.</span><span class='zh'>人工智能让人们可以在一个周末轻松构建习惯追踪器、待办事项和笔记应用等小型软件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI has made it easy to build small applications over a weekend, such as habit trackers, to-do lists, and note-taking tools. The author argues that more of this energy should go toward improving the open-source software people already rely on, making it faster, lighter, and easier to run. The point is not to discourage people from building, but to redirect some of that effort toward shared infrastructure.</span><span class='zh'>人工智能让人们可以在一个周末轻松构建习惯追踪器、待办事项和笔记应用等小型软件。作者认为，更多这类精力应投入到改进人们已经依赖的开源软件上，让它们更快、更轻量、更易运行。重点不是阻止人们开发新东西，而是将部分努力转向共享基础设施。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span><span class='tag' data-t='Energy' style='--c:#eab308'>Energy</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/17/top_stories_11172026'><span class='en'>Nov 17</span><span class='zh'>11月17日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>3.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>344</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50532086' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>234</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50463399" data-id="50463399" data-tags="AI|Programming|Hardware" data-score="341" data-comments="172" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50463399" data-time="1794461364">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=WPj3aQ-xxpI" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=youtube.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>youtube.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">88</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=WPj3aQ-xxpI" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Code and Codex reverse engineered my 1994 Jeep&#x27;s ECU over a $3 Arduino [video]</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 和 Codex 用一块 3 美元 Arduino 逆向工程了我 1994 年 Jeep 的 ECU</span></a><span class="dom">youtube.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude Code and Codex were used to reverse engineer the ECU of a 1994 Jeep.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 和 Codex 被用于逆向工程一辆 1994 年 Jeep 的 ECU。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Claude Code and Codex were used to reverse engineer the ECU of a 1994 Jeep. The project used a $3 Arduino as part of the setup. It is presented as a video demonstration of AI-assisted automotive reverse engineering.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 和 Codex 被用于逆向工程一辆 1994 年 Jeep 的 ECU。该项目使用了一块 3 美元的 Arduino 作为设备的一部分。相关成果以视频形式展示了 AI 辅助的汽车系统逆向工程过程。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.breck-mckye.com/blog/2026/11/software-is-breaking-down-at-a-systemic-level/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Software is breaking down at a systemic level</span><span class='zh'>软件正在系统性崩坏</span></a><span class="dom">breck-mckye.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author argues that recent failures point to software problems at a systemic level.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为，近期的多起故障表明软件正面临系统性问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author argues that recent failures point to software problems at a systemic level. The examples cited are a two-day global Microsoft outage and an FAA ground stop that halted all flights in the United States. These events are presented as a recent combination of major technology and infrastructure disruptions.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为，近期的多起故障表明软件正面临系统性问题。文章举出的例子包括持续两天的全球微软服务中断，以及导致美国所有航班停飞的 FAA 地面停飞令。这些事件被视为近期重大技术和基础设施中断的集中体现。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theverge.com/tech/1012372/tv-privacy-acr-questions" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I asked every TV manufacturer 17 questions about how they spy on you</span><span class='zh'>我向每家电视制造商提出了 17 个关于其如何监视用户的问题</span></a><span class="dom">theverge.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The investigation sent 17 yes-or-no privacy questions to 11 TV and streaming-device companies, but only a few answered every question.</span><span class='zh'>这项调查向 11 家电视和流媒体设备公司提出了 17 个是非题式的隐私问题，但只有少数公司回答了全部问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The investigation sent 17 yes-or-no privacy questions to 11 TV and streaming-device companies, but only a few answered every question. The questions covered issues such as recording or transcribing audio, taking screenshots, storing sensitive information, and sharing data with the cloud. The effort followed accusations involving LG and was intended to obtain clearer answers than lengthy privacy policies provide.</span><span class='zh'>这项调查向 11 家电视和流媒体设备公司提出了 17 个是非题式的隐私问题，但只有少数公司回答了全部问题。问题涉及录音或保存音频转录、截取屏幕、在电视上存储敏感信息，以及与云端共享数据等事项。调查起因之一是针对 LG 的监视指控，目的是获得比冗长隐私政策更清晰的回答。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>338</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50453474' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>237</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50475713" data-id="50475713" data-tags="AI|Open Source" data-score="338" data-comments="104" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50475713" data-time="1794524777">
<a class="thumb" href="https://edge0.ai/blog/introducing-edge0-145b-frontier-ai-capabilities-on-your-own-computer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://cdn.hashnode.com/uploads/covers/6aea03219fa4aa99923b5acf/de5ebdea-3436-4b9a-b3f2-e9910f3a0abc.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='edge0.ai' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://edge0.ai/blog/introducing-edge0-145b-frontier-ai-capabilities-on-your-own-computer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Edge0-145B: Frontier AI Capabilities on Your Own Computer</span><span class='zh'>Edge0-145B：在自己的电脑上运行前沿 AI 能力</span></a><span class="dom">edge0.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Edge0 has open-sourced Edge0-145B and Edge0-120B, along with an inference framework and deployment tools.</span><span class='zh'>Edge0 开源了 Edge0-145B 和 Edge0-120B，同时发布推理框架和部署工具。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Edge0 has open-sourced Edge0-145B and Edge0-120B, along with an inference framework and deployment tools. Edge0-145B is described as an open-source, on-device model with 145 billion parameters for coding, reasoning, and agent tasks. Local inference avoids cloud-model token fees, but large models still require substantial memory, bandwidth, and power.</span><span class='zh'>Edge0 开源了 Edge0-145B 和 Edge0-120B，同时发布推理框架和部署工具。Edge0-145B 被称为首个开源的 1450 亿参数设备端模型，可用于编程、推理和智能体任务。本地推理无需支付云模型的 token 费用，但大型模型仍需要大量内存、带宽和电力。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>338</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50475713' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>104</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50503255" data-id="50503255" data-tags="Privacy|Legal" data-score="338" data-comments="122" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50503255" data-time="1794708054">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/11/were-asking-appeals-court-require-warrant-alpr-searches" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/siren_v._san_jose.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='eff.org' data-c='#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">92</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/11/were-asking-appeals-court-require-warrant-alpr-searches" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We&#x27;re Asking an Appeals Court to Require a Warrant for ALPR Searches</span><span class='zh'>我们请求上诉法院要求 ALPR 搜索取得搜查令</span></a><span class="dom">eff.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The request asks an appeals court to require a warrant for searches using automated license plate readers.</span><span class='zh'>该请求要求上诉法院规定，使用自动车牌识别系统进行搜索必须取得搜查令。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The request asks an appeals court to require a warrant for searches using automated license plate readers. The argument is that ALPR systems can let law enforcement review a person&#x27;s past movements, rather than merely observe one license plate on a passing car. Their ability to search historical location records is therefore presented as materially different from ordinary roadside observation.</span><span class='zh'>该请求要求上诉法院规定，使用自动车牌识别系统进行搜索必须取得搜查令。其理由是，ALPR 能让执法部门查看某人的历史移动轨迹，而不仅是观察一辆经过车辆的单个车牌。因而，搜索历史位置记录的能力被认为与普通路边观察存在实质差异。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/15/top_stories_11152026'><span class='en'>Nov 15</span><span class='zh'>11月15日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>338</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50503255' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>122</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50532191" data-id="50532191" data-tags="AI" data-score="337" data-comments="118" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50532191" data-time="1794927223">
<a class="thumb" href="https://runta.com/blog/introducing-frontierharness-eval-v1-1/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://runta.com/social/blog-introducing-frontierharness-eval-v1-1.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='runta.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">93</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://runta.com/blog/introducing-frontierharness-eval-v1-1/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What is the best harness for each model?</span><span class='zh'>每种模型最适合什么 harness？</span></a><span class="dom">runta.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>FrontierHarness Eval v1.1 tested 22 coding-agent harnesses with five models across the same 62 tasks, repeating each test four times for 27,280 runs.</span><span class='zh'>FrontierHarness Eval v1.1 使用 5 个模型，在相同的 62 项任务上测试了 22 个编码代理 harness，每项测试重复 4 次，共运行 27,280 次。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>FrontierHarness Eval v1.1 tested 22 coding-agent harnesses with five models across the same 62 tasks, repeating each test four times for 27,280 runs. The best-value harness varied by model, and cost rankings also shifted substantially between models. With the model held constant, the most expensive harness cost 1.8 to 5 times more per task than the cheapest. The results suggest that changing models may also require reconsidering the harness.</span><span class='zh'>FrontierHarness Eval v1.1 使用 5 个模型，在相同的 62 项任务上测试了 22 个编码代理 harness，每项测试重复 4 次，共运行 27,280 次。性价比最高的 harness 因模型而异，成本排名也会随模型大幅变化。在固定模型的情况下，最贵 harness 的单任务成本是最便宜者的 1.8 至 5 倍。结果表明，更换模型时可能也需要重新评估所用的 harness。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/17/top_stories_11172026'><span class='en'>Nov 17</span><span class='zh'>11月17日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>3.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>337</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50532191' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>118</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50522643" data-id="50522643" data-tags="Science" data-score="337" data-comments="138" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50522643" data-time="1794860538">

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece is titled “Chemistry is Actually Geometry.” It presents chemistry through the lens of geometry.</span><span class='zh'>文章题为《化学其实是几何学》。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece is titled “Chemistry is Actually Geometry.” It presents chemistry through the lens of geometry.</span><span class='zh'>文章题为《化学其实是几何学》。它从几何学的角度看待化学。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/16/top_stories_11162026'><span class='en'>Nov 16</span><span class='zh'>11月16日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>337</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50522643' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>138</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50451903" data-id="50451903" data-tags="Hardware|Security" data-score="336" data-comments="131" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50451903" data-time="1794394120">
<a class="thumb" href="https://blog.kate.land/posts/using-voltage-glitching-to-unlock-the-a100/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://blog.kate.land/images/ga100-block-diagram.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='blog.kate.land' data-c='#f59e0b' data-tag='Hardware'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">95</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.kate.land/posts/using-voltage-glitching-to-unlock-the-a100/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Using voltage glitching to unleash the A100 in my mining card</span><span class='zh'>通过电压故障注入解锁挖矿卡中的 A100</span></a><span class="dom">blog.kate.land</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A voltage fault-injection vulnerability in the GA100 die can reportedly blow arbitrary fuses on an NVIDIA CMP 170HX.</span><span class='zh'>据称，GA100 芯片中的电压故障注入漏洞可以烧断任意熔丝。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A voltage fault-injection vulnerability in the GA100 die can reportedly blow arbitrary fuses on an NVIDIA CMP 170HX. The author used it to turn an 8GB CMP 170HX into a card functionally similar to an A100-80G, enabling roughly 120–126 functional SMs instead of the 108 used in production A100s. The method can also unlock features including NVLink, PCIe Gen4, NVJPG, NVDEC, and MIG, although several of those features were not specifically tested.</span><span class='zh'>据称，GA100 芯片中的电压故障注入漏洞可以烧断任意熔丝。作者利用这一点，将一张 8GB 的 NVIDIA CMP 170HX 改造成在功能上类似 A100-80G 的显卡，并启用了约 120 至 126 个可用 SM，而量产 A100 使用的是 108 个。该方法还可以解锁 NVLink、PCIe Gen4、NVJPG、NVDEC 和 MIG 等功能，但其中几项尚未经过专门测试。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>336</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50451903' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>131</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50497009" data-id="50497009" data-tags="Privacy|Legal" data-score="336" data-comments="223" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50497009" data-time="1794664086">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.reuters.com/world/italy-fines-meta-887-million-over-android-users-data-access-2026-11-14/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=reuters.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>reuters.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">96</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.reuters.com/world/italy-fines-meta-887-million-over-android-users-data-access-2026-11-14/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Italy fines Meta $887M over Android users&#x27; data access</span><span class='zh'>意大利因 Android 用户数据访问问题对 Meta 罚款 8.87 亿美元</span></a><span class="dom">reuters.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Italy fined Meta $887 million over Android users’ data access.</span><span class='zh'>意大利因 Android 用户数据访问问题对 Meta 处以 8.87 亿美元罚款。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Italy fined Meta $887 million over Android users’ data access. The case concerns Meta’s handling of data access involving users of Android devices.</span><span class='zh'>意大利因 Android 用户数据访问问题对 Meta 处以 8.87 亿美元罚款。案件涉及 Meta 如何处理 Android 设备用户的数据访问。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://teorth.github.io/tao-web/six-math-essentials.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Six Math Essentials</span><span class='zh'>Terence Tao 的六个数学要点</span></a><span class="dom">teorth.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>In Six Math Essentials, Fields Medalist Terence Tao introduces six concepts that span mathematical thinking from antiquity to the modern frontier.</span><span class='zh'>菲尔兹奖得主 Terence Tao 在《六个数学要点》中介绍了贯穿古代至现代数学前沿的六个核心概念。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>In Six Math Essentials, Fields Medalist Terence Tao introduces six concepts that span mathematical thinking from antiquity to the modern frontier. The book covers numbers, algebra, geometry, probability, analysis, and dynamics as gateways to quantitative thought, abstraction, calculation, uncertainty, scale, and change. No errata have been recorded for the book.</span><span class='zh'>菲尔兹奖得主 Terence Tao 在《六个数学要点》中介绍了贯穿古代至现代数学前沿的六个核心概念。全书分别以数字、代数、几何、概率、分析和动力学为入口，讨论定量思维、抽象、计算、不确定性、尺度与变化。目前该书尚无勘误记录。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://hugovk.dev/blog/2026/lock-github-actions/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Lock Your GitHub Actions</span><span class='zh'>锁定你的 GitHub Actions</span></a><span class="dom">hugovk.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GitHub Actions now supports workflow lockfiles.</span><span class='zh'>GitHub Actions 现在支持工作流锁定文件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GitHub Actions now supports workflow lockfiles. Previously, users were advised to pin actions to a full Git commit SHA because tags can be changed and redirected to malicious code in supply-chain attacks. Lockfiles provide another mechanism for constraining the actions used by a workflow.</span><span class='zh'>GitHub Actions 现在支持工作流锁定文件。此前，建议用户将 action 固定到完整的 Git 提交 SHA，因为标签可以被修改，攻击者可能借此在供应链攻击中将原本安全的标签指向恶意代码。锁定文件为限制工作流所使用的 actions 提供了另一种机制。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.cornell.edu/stories/2026/10/sheep-grazing-solar-fields-offers-farmers-economic-ecological-gains" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Grazing sheep in fields with solar arrays</span><span class='zh'>在太阳能阵列田地放牧绵羊</span></a><span class="dom">news.cornell.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Two studies examine the growing practice of grazing sheep beneath solar arrays.</span><span class='zh'>两项研究考察了在太阳能阵列下放牧绵羊这一日益普及的做法。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Two studies examine the growing practice of grazing sheep beneath solar arrays. One found that grazing at two sheep per hectare increased wild bees, honey bees, and lady beetles by 38% compared with mowing, driven mainly by an 83% rise in honey bees. The other study describes a decision-support tool to help farmers expand solar-grazing herds profitably.</span><span class='zh'>两项研究考察了在太阳能阵列下放牧绵羊这一日益普及的做法。其中一项发现，每公顷放牧两只绵羊时，野生蜜蜂、蜜蜂和瓢虫的数量比割草对照地增加 38%，主要原因是蜜蜂数量增加了 83%。另一项研究介绍了一种决策支持工具，帮助农民以盈利方式扩大太阳能牧羊规模。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span><span class='tag' data-t='Energy' style='--c:#eab308'>Energy</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/16/top_stories_11162026'><span class='en'>Nov 16</span><span class='zh'>11月16日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>324</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50520182' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>331</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50454625" data-id="50454625" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|AI|Code" data-score="285" data-comments="111" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50454625" data-time="1794411186">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/FrigadeHQ/yogurt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1453132706/355ef15b-b53b-4211-bd76-47cdaad9ef5b' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='github.com' data-c='#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/FrigadeHQ/yogurt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Yogurt – Free, private, open-source alternative to Granola</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Yogurt——免费、私密的开源 Granola 替代品</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Yogurt is a free, MIT-licensed desktop app that uses on-device AI models to transcribe, summarize, and chat about meetings.</span><span class='zh'>Yogurt 是一款采用 MIT 许可证的免费桌面应用，使用设备本地的 AI 模型转录会议、生成摘要并进行问答。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Yogurt is a free, MIT-licensed desktop app that uses on-device AI models to transcribe, summarize, and chat about meetings. It runs in the background without joining calls, and keeps transcripts, recordings, and summaries on the user’s computer. The app also offers calendar integration and a local MCP server for optionally sharing notes with external frontier models.</span><span class='zh'>Yogurt 是一款采用 MIT 许可证的免费桌面应用，使用设备本地的 AI 模型转录会议、生成摘要并进行问答。它在后台运行，不会加入通话，会议转录、录音和摘要也不会离开用户的电脑。该应用还提供日历集成和本地 MCP 服务器，用户可以选择通过它将笔记分享给外部前沿模型。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>285</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50454625' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>111</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50497589" data-id="50497589" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|Code" data-score="272" data-comments="27" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50497589" data-time="1794668655">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Vaibhaav-Tiwari/deepseek-v3-jax" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: DeepSeek V3 in ~200 lines of pure Jax</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：用约 200 行纯 JAX 实现 DeepSeek V3</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A project implements the DeepSeek-V3 architecture in 190 lines of pure JAX, including MLA, DeepSeekMoE, auxiliary-loss-free balancing, and multi-token prediction.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目用 190 行纯 JAX 实现了 DeepSeek-V3 架构，包括 MLA、DeepSeekMoE、无辅助损失均衡和多词元预测。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A project implements the DeepSeek-V3 architecture in 190 lines of pure JAX, including MLA, DeepSeekMoE, auxiliary-loss-free balancing, and multi-token prediction. The model was pretrained from scratch on a MacBook Air using public-domain detective fiction, without PyTorch, Flax, or Equinox. Its parameters are plain dictionaries of arrays, and the implementation is organized as readable functions.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目用 190 行纯 JAX 实现了 DeepSeek-V3 架构，包括 MLA、DeepSeekMoE、无辅助损失均衡和多词元预测。该模型在 MacBook Air 上使用公版侦探小说从头预训练，未使用 PyTorch、Flax 或 Equinox。模型参数采用由数组组成的普通字典，代码则组织为可从头读到尾的函数。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/jquery/jquery/releases/tag/4.1.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>jQuery 4.1.0</span><span class='zh'>jQuery 4.1.0</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>jQuery 4.1.0 was released, with the release notes listing an Ajax change that adds a responseURL field to jqXHR.</span><span class='zh'>jQuery 4.1.0 已发布，更新说明列出了一项 Ajax 改动：为 jqXHR 添加 responseURL 字段。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>jQuery 4.1.0 was released, with the release notes listing an Ajax change that adds a responseURL field to jqXHR. The same announcement also references a jQuery 3.8.0 release. The update continues development of the JavaScript library across its newer and older release lines.</span><span class='zh'>jQuery 4.1.0 已发布，更新说明列出了一项 Ajax 改动：为 jqXHR 添加 responseURL 字段。同一公告还提到了 jQuery 3.8.0 的发布。此次更新同时推进了该 JavaScript 库的新旧版本线。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/microsoft/Procexp-Mac" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Process Explorer for macOS</span><span class='zh'>适用于 macOS 的 Process Explorer</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft’s Process Explorer for Mac is a native macOS implementation of the core Process Explorer experience.</span><span class='zh'>微软的 macOS 版 Process Explorer 是对核心 Process Explorer 体验的原生实现。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft’s Process Explorer for Mac is a native macOS implementation of the core Process Explorer experience. It provides a live process tree, module, handle, and thread inspection, per-process properties, system-wide performance graphs, and code-signing checks. The Swift, SwiftUI, and AppKit application uses public macOS APIs, with optional support for a privileged helper.</span><span class='zh'>微软的 macOS 版 Process Explorer 是对核心 Process Explorer 体验的原生实现。它提供实时进程树、模块、句柄和线程检查、逐进程属性、系统级性能图表以及代码签名检查。该应用使用 Swift、SwiftUI 和 AppKit 构建，并通过 macOS 公共 API 工作，还可选择使用特权辅助程序。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/lonewolf-im/lonewolf/releases/tag/v0.3.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Lonewolf 0.3 – A standalone XMPP server written in Rust</span><span class='zh'>Lonewolf 0.3：用 Rust 编写的独立 XMPP 服务器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Lonewolf 0.3.0 adds certificate-authenticated federation, direct TLS, and additional desktop-client features.</span><span class='zh'>Lonewolf 0.3.0 新增基于证书认证的联邦通信、直接 TLS，以及更多桌面客户端功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Lonewolf 0.3.0 adds certificate-authenticated federation, direct TLS, and additional desktop-client features. It remains a pre-1.0, single-node server, while full XMPP compliance and clustering are still on the roadmap. The release expands secure federation without yet providing those larger deployment features.</span><span class='zh'>Lonewolf 0.3.0 新增基于证书认证的联邦通信、直接 TLS，以及更多桌面客户端功能。它仍是一个处于 1.0 之前阶段的单节点服务器，完整 XMPP 兼容性和集群支持仍在路线图中。此次更新增强了安全联邦能力，但尚未提供这些更大规模部署功能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/IRS-Public/direct-file" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>US IRS free online tax filing open source repo (2025)</span><span class='zh'>美国国税局免费在线报税开源仓库（2025）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Direct File is a US government service that lets taxpayers file federal returns electronically for free and directly with the IRS.</span><span class='zh'>Direct File 是美国政府提供的服务，允许纳税人免费通过电子方式直接向美国国税局提交联邦税表。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Direct File is a US government service that lets taxpayers file federal returns electronically for free and directly with the IRS. It uses interview-style questions, works across phones and computers, supports English and Spanish, and translates users&#x27; answers into tax forms submitted through the IRS Modernized e-File API. The repository contains the service&#x27;s code and related tests and scenarios.</span><span class='zh'>Direct File 是美国政府提供的服务，允许纳税人免费通过电子方式直接向美国国税局提交联邦税表。该服务采用问答式界面，可在手机和电脑等设备上使用，支持英语和西班牙语，并将用户答案转换为税表后通过国税局 Modernized e-File API 提交。该开源仓库包含服务代码以及相关测试和场景。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/14/top_stories_11142026'><span class='en'>Nov 14</span><span class='zh'>11月14日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>148</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50499083' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>91</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50460154" data-id="50460154" data-tags="Hardware|Programming|Code" data-score="140" data-comments="57" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50460154" data-time="1794438158">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/tylertms/Stela" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A general purpose Apple Silicon layer for x86-64 PCs</span><span class='zh'>面向 x86-64 PC 的通用 Apple Silicon 层</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Stela is a UEFI boot program intended to provide an Apple Silicon layer for x86-64 PCs.</span><span class='zh'>Stela 是一个面向 x86-64 PC、旨在提供 Apple Silicon 兼容层的 UEFI 启动程序。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Stela is a UEFI boot program intended to provide an Apple Silicon layer for x86-64 PCs. It includes an Arm64-to-x86-64 translator, an NVIDIA Metal driver, and a model of Apple silicon hardware, and can currently boot macOS and most apps. The v0.1.0 release is slow, requires more testing, and has a low chance of booting on current hardware.</span><span class='zh'>Stela 是一个面向 x86-64 PC、旨在提供 Apple Silicon 兼容层的 UEFI 启动程序。它包含 Arm64 到 x86-64 的转换器、NVIDIA Metal 驱动以及 Apple 芯片硬件模型，目前可以启动 macOS 和大多数应用。v0.1.0 运行速度较慢，仍需大量测试，在当前硬件上成功启动的概率也很低。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>140</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50460154' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>57</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50449891" data-id="50449891" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|AI|Code" data-score="138" data-comments="60" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50449891" data-time="1794373007">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/adrbn/bo2-mac" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Black Ops II statically recompiled to native ARM64 macOS</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：将《使命召唤：黑色行动 II》静态重编译为原生 ARM64 macOS 应用</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This project statically recompiles a Steam copy of Black Ops II into a native ARM64 macOS application using Claude Opus 5.5, without Wine, Crossover, Rosetta, or another emulator.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目使用 Claude Opus 5.5，将 Steam 版《使命召唤：黑色行动 II》静态重编译为原生 ARM64 macOS 应用，不依赖 Wine、Crossover、Rosetta 或其他模拟器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This project statically recompiles a Steam copy of Black Ops II into a native ARM64 macOS application using Claude Opus 5.5, without Wine, Crossover, Rosetta, or another emulator. The game reportedly runs at nearly 60 frames per second across the campaign, local multiplayer with bots, and local Zombies mode, including owned DLC maps. Split-screen is being worked on, while LAN mode is planned for later.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目使用 Claude Opus 5.5，将 Steam 版《使命召唤：黑色行动 II》静态重编译为原生 ARM64 macOS 应用，不依赖 Wine、Crossover、Rosetta 或其他模拟器。据称，战役、本地机器人多人模式和本地僵尸模式都能以接近 60 帧运行，已拥有的 DLC 地图也可使用。开发者正在处理分屏功能，局域网模式则计划稍后加入。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gitlab.com/snipedbywifi/terminactory" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: A terminal-native idle factory game, written in Rust with ratatui</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：用 Rust 和 ratatui 编写的终端原生放置工厂游戏</span></a><span class="dom">gitlab.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Term inactory is a cozy factory game that runs directly in a terminal and was built for playing while waiting on long-running agent tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Term inactory 是一款直接运行在终端中的休闲工厂游戏，适合在等待耗时较长的代理任务时游玩。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Term inactory is a cozy factory game that runs directly in a terminal and was built for playing while waiting on long-running agent tasks. It includes basic factory mechanics such as building, deleting, selecting, measuring earnings, and researching unlocks. Additional features include a fictional browser with a wiki and forums, extensive lore, and hooks that display coding-agent responses as in-game “telegrams.”</span><span class='zh'>Term inactory 是一款直接运行在终端中的休闲工厂游戏，适合在等待耗时较长的代理任务时游玩。游戏包含建造、删除、选择、收益测算和研究解锁等基本工厂机制。它还加入了带有 Wiki 和论坛的虚构浏览器、大量背景故事，以及将编码代理回复显示为游戏内“电报”的钩子功能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Parcha-ai/agentrun/blob/main/README.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: AgentRun – open-source, Self-improving Managed Agents</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：AgentRun——开源、自我改进的托管智能体</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GREP.AI has open-sourced AgentRun as a managed platform for agents.</span><span class='zh'>GREP.AI 将 AgentRun 开源为一个托管智能体平台。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GREP.AI has open-sourced AgentRun as a managed platform for agents. It includes self-improving agents, web-grounded workflows, sandboxes, computer environments, tools and integrations, and behavioral analytics. AgentRun supports harnesses including Pi, Claude Agents, Codex, and OpenCode, and can be self-hosted or used as a hosted service.</span><span class='zh'>GREP.AI 将 AgentRun 开源为一个托管智能体平台。它包含自我改进智能体、基于网页信息的工作流、沙箱和计算机环境、工具与集成，以及智能体行为分析。AgentRun 支持 Pi、Claude Agents、Codex 和 OpenCode 等 harness，也可自行托管或使用托管版本。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>134</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50471650' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>37</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50458166" data-id="50458166" data-tags="AI|Programming|Code" data-score="132" data-comments="31" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50458166" data-time="1794428637">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/huawei-csl/MACaQ" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Huawei releases MACaQ, its internal quantizer for local inference</span><span class='zh'>华为发布用于本地推理的内部量化器 MACaQ</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>MACaQ is a model-agnostic quantization system for GGUF and local inference across text, image, video, and audio models.</span><span class='zh'>MACaQ 是一个面向 GGUF 和本地推理的模型无关量化系统，支持文本、图像、视频和音频模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>MACaQ is a model-agnostic quantization system for GGUF and local inference across text, image, video, and audio models. It supports llama.cpp, vLLM, Diffusers, and PyTorch. Huawei says it can quantize a 122-billion-parameter model in 64 minutes using 32.5 GB of device memory, while achieving lower perplexity than MR-GPTQ and DuQuant++ in stated comparisons.</span><span class='zh'>MACaQ 是一个面向 GGUF 和本地推理的模型无关量化系统，支持文本、图像、视频和音频模型。它兼容 llama.cpp、vLLM、Diffusers 和 PyTorch。华为称，MACaQ 可使用 32.5 GB 设备内存，在 64 分钟内完成一个 1220 亿参数模型的量化；在其给出的比较中，困惑度低于 MR-GPTQ 和 DuQuant++。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>132</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50458166' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>31</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50539610" data-id="50539610" data-tags="Open Source|Programming|Code" data-score="129" data-comments="32" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50539610" data-time="1794957529">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/microsoft/onnxruntime" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microsoft&#x27;s ONNX Runtime has disappeared from GitHub</span><span class='zh'>微软 ONNX Runtime 从 GitHub 消失</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft&#x27;s ONNX Runtime has disappeared from GitHub.</span><span class='zh'>微软的 ONNX Runtime 已从 GitHub 消失。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft&#x27;s ONNX Runtime has disappeared from GitHub. The available information does not establish why it disappeared, when the change occurred, or what will happen to the project.</span><span class='zh'>微软的 ONNX Runtime 已从 GitHub 消失。现有信息无法确认其消失原因、发生时间或项目后续安排。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/gmkurtzer/ginux" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Greg Kurtzer&#x27;s Ginux is now a userspace kernel</span><span class='zh'>Greg Kurtzer 的 Ginux 现已成为用户态内核</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ginux is now a configurable userspace kernel for isolated Linux compatibility instances.</span><span class='zh'>Ginux 现已成为用于隔离 Linux 兼容实例的可配置用户态内核。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ginux is now a configurable userspace kernel for isolated Linux compatibility instances. It runs as an ordinary Linux process and defines an explicit, versioned contract between managed workloads and the host kernel. Its earlier phase as an independent Rust kernel booting through UEFI is complete, with that source preserved separately while active development continues on the userspace version.</span><span class='zh'>Ginux 现已成为用于隔离 Linux 兼容实例的可配置用户态内核。它作为普通 Linux 进程运行，并在受管理工作负载与主机内核之间建立明确且有版本控制的契约。此前通过 UEFI 启动、以 Rust 编写的独立内核阶段已经结束，相关源代码被单独保留，当前开发转向用户态版本。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>114</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50450007' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>76</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50496787" data-id="50496787" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|Web|Data|Education|Code" data-score="112" data-comments="68" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50496787" data-time="1794662230">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ZigaoWang/zigzag" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Zigzag, a native Mac browser for people leaving Arc</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Zigzag——为离开 Arc 的用户打造的原生 Mac 浏览器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Zigzag is a free, open-source Mac browser written in Swift and based on WebKit, created by a high-school student from Shanghai.</span><span class='zh'>Zigzag 是一款由上海高中生开发的免费开源 Mac 浏览器，使用 Swift 编写并基于 WebKit。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Zigzag is a free, open-source Mac browser written in Swift and based on WebKit, created by a high-school student from Shanghai. It aims to provide an Arc-like interface and can import spaces, pins, history, passwords, and cookies from Arc or other browsers. Despite using WebKit, it supports Chrome extensions, including 1Password, Bitwarden, and Tampermonkey. The browser requires no account and claims to have no telemetry or analytics.</span><span class='zh'>Zigzag 是一款由上海高中生开发的免费开源 Mac 浏览器，使用 Swift 编写并基于 WebKit。它试图提供类似 Arc 的界面，并可从 Arc 或其他浏览器导入空间、固定项目、历史记录、密码和 Cookie。虽然基于 WebKit，它仍支持 Chrome 扩展，包括 1Password、Bitwarden 和 Tampermonkey。该浏览器无需账户，并声称不包含遥测或分析功能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/eclipse-mosquitto/mosquitto" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Eclipse Mosquitto – An open source MQTT broker</span><span class='zh'>Eclipse Mosquitto：开源 MQTT 代理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Eclipse Mosquitto is an open-source implementation of an MQTT broker supporting MQTT versions 5.0, 3.1.1, and 3.1.</span><span class='zh'>Eclipse Mosquitto 是一个开源 MQTT 代理实现，支持 MQTT 5.0、3.1.1 和 3.1 版本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Eclipse Mosquitto is an open-source implementation of an MQTT broker supporting MQTT versions 5.0, 3.1.1, and 3.1. It also provides C and C++ client libraries and command-line tools for publishing, subscribing, and administering the broker.</span><span class='zh'>Eclipse Mosquitto 是一个开源 MQTT 代理实现，支持 MQTT 5.0、3.1.1 和 3.1 版本。它还提供 C 和 C++ 客户端库，以及用于发布、订阅和管理代理的命令行工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/chocoford/ExcalidrawZ" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ExcalidrawZ: Local Native Excalidraw on macOS/iOS</span><span class='zh'>ExcalidrawZ：macOS/iOS 原生本地版 Excalidraw</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ExcalidrawZ is a macOS and iOS drawing app built with SwiftUI that preserves the familiar Excalidraw canvas.</span><span class='zh'>ExcalidrawZ 是一款使用 SwiftUI 构建的 macOS 和 iOS 绘图应用，保留了熟悉的 Excalidraw 画布。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ExcalidrawZ is a macOS and iOS drawing app built with SwiftUI that preserves the familiar Excalidraw canvas. It adds native document management, iCloud sync, history, export, AI assistance, MCP integration, and other Apple-platform features. Drawings are handled as application documents rather than loose browser downloads.</span><span class='zh'>ExcalidrawZ 是一款使用 SwiftUI 构建的 macOS 和 iOS 绘图应用，保留了熟悉的 Excalidraw 画布。它增加了原生文件管理、iCloud 同步、历史记录、导出、AI 辅助、MCP 集成及其他 Apple 平台功能。绘图会作为应用文档管理，而不是浏览器中零散下载的文件。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/0xdeadf1sh/T1DMAI" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I have trained 15 blood glucose forecasting models on my own data</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：我用自己的数据训练了 15 个血糖预测模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project is a model zoo for forecasting blood glucose levels, trained and tested on the author’s personal data as well as the public Metabonet dataset.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目是一个血糖预测模型集合，使用作者自己的数据和公开的 Metabonet 数据集进行训练与测试。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project is a model zoo for forecasting blood glucose levels, trained and tested on the author’s personal data as well as the public Metabonet dataset. It includes 15 forecasting models, including models developed by the author. Most of the repository’s code was written with AI assistance.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目是一个血糖预测模型集合，使用作者自己的数据和公开的 Metabonet 数据集进行训练与测试。集合中包含 15 个预测模型，其中也有作者自行开发的模型。仓库中的大部分代码由 AI 辅助编写。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/0xdeadf1sh/AssemblyForNeovim" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Godbolt-in-a-floating-window for Neovim (C, C++, Rust)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Neovim 浮动窗口中的 Godbolt（支持 C、C++、Rust）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AssemblyForNeovim brings a Godbolt-style assembly view into a floating Neovim window for C, C++, and Rust.</span><span class='zh'>AssemblyForNeovim 将类似 Godbolt 的汇编查看功能带入 Neovim 浮动窗口，支持 C、C++ 和 Rust。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AssemblyForNeovim brings a Godbolt-style assembly view into a floating Neovim window for C, C++, and Rust. It generates assembly with a compiler installed locally, so it does not require an internet connection.</span><span class='zh'>AssemblyForNeovim 将类似 Godbolt 的汇编查看功能带入 Neovim 浮动窗口，支持 C、C++ 和 Rust。它使用本地安装的编译器生成汇编代码，因此无需互联网连接。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ages2001/WINUDF" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Winudf – DVD / BluRay driver for DOS, Windows 1.x – 3x / 95 / 98 / ME</span><span class='zh'>Winudf：适用于 DOS、Windows 1.x–3.x、95、98、ME 的 DVD/Blu-ray 驱动</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>WINUDF is an installable file-system driver that lets DOS and early Windows systems read and write UDF media.</span><span class='zh'>WINUDF 是一种可安装文件系统驱动，让 DOS 和早期 Windows 系统能够读写 UDF 媒体。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>WINUDF is an installable file-system driver that lets DOS and early Windows systems read and write UDF media. It supports UDF versions 1.02 through 2.60, including DVDs, Blu-ray discs, packet-written CDs, and UDF-formatted USB drives. DOS and Windows 1.0x–3.11 receive drive letters through WINUDF.EXE, while WINUDF.VXD provides a long-name file-system driver for Windows 95, 98, and ME.</span><span class='zh'>WINUDF 是一种可安装文件系统驱动，让 DOS 和早期 Windows 系统能够读写 UDF 媒体。它支持 1.02 至 2.60 版 UDF，涵盖 DVD、蓝光光盘、包写入 CD 以及采用 UDF 格式的 USB 驱动器。DOS 和 Windows 1.0x–3.11 可通过 WINUDF.EXE 获得盘符，Windows 95、98 和 ME 则使用支持长文件名的 WINUDF.VXD 驱动。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/16/top_stories_11162026'><span class='en'>Nov 16</span><span class='zh'>11月16日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>95</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50515182' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>16</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50497147" data-id="50497147" data-tags="Show HN|Web|Hardware|Code" data-score="87" data-comments="26" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50497147" data-time="1794665292">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/paul-nameless/agweb" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Agweb – a browser for agents that stays out of your way</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Agweb——不妨碍用户操作的代理浏览器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Agweb is a macOS browser for coding agents, built on WebKit and designed not to steal keyboard focus.</span><span class='zh'>Agweb 是一款面向编码代理的 macOS 浏览器，基于 WebKit 构建，设计目标是不抢占键盘焦点。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Agweb is a macOS browser for coding agents, built on WebKit and designed not to steal keyboard focus. Agents control the single window from the shell through an agweb command modeled on Playwright CLI, while users can watch all tabs live in one view. Idle tabs are suspended; the author reports that five open pages used 0% CPU on a Mac, compared with five to seven busy cores from Chrome’s GPU process.</span><span class='zh'>Agweb 是一款面向编码代理的 macOS 浏览器，基于 WebKit 构建，设计目标是不抢占键盘焦点。代理通过类似 Playwright CLI 的 agweb 命令从 shell 控制这个窗口，用户则可以在一个视图中实时查看所有标签页。闲置标签页会被挂起；作者称，在 Mac 上打开五个页面时其 CPU 占用为 0%，而 Chrome 的 GPU 进程会占用五到七个繁忙核心。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/14/top_stories_11142026'><span class='en'>Nov 14</span><span class='zh'>11月14日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>87</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50497147' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>26</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50460263" data-id="50460263" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="82" data-comments="14" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50460263" data-time="1794438567">
